           Quantity and remaining quantity
           Location
          Stock Issue Policy
          Use FEFO - First Expiry First Out - where applicable.

           Actual Event Cost - real consumed operational cost
           Historical values are never overwritten

      48. Cost Allocation Engine
      One bill or purchase may be distributed across events and overheads.
           One Event
           Multiple Events
           Event + Overhead
           Multiple Branches
           Use allocation lines with controlled percentages/amounts
          Example
          ₹10,000 expense -> EVT-1001 40% + EVT-1002 35% + General Overhead 25%




      49. Event Profitability
           Revenue = final approved event revenue
           Direct Costs: actual food, outside food, temporary labour, permanent event labour allocation, transport,
            fuel, rental, packing, disposables, wastage and other direct event expenses
           Event Contribution = Revenue - Direct Cost
           Net Event Profit = Event Contribution - Allocated Overhead
           Display both contribution profit and net profit


      50. Event Close

           Stage 6 - Provisional P&L
           Stage 7 - Financial Close
           Reopening requires approval + reason

                           14                                  ->                       Inventory Reservation

                           15                                  ->                       Purchase Requirement

                           16                                  ->                       PO / GRN / Quality

                           17                                  ->                       Stock

                           18                                  ->                       Production

                           19                                  ->                       Yield

                           20                                  ->                       Packing

                           21                                  ->                       Dispatch

                           22                                  ->                       Live Service

                           23                                  ->                       Live Guest Count

                           24                                  ->                       Customer Extras

                           25                                  ->                       Returns / Wastage

                           26                                  ->                       Staff / Vendor / Vehicle Settlement

                           27                                  ->                       Final Invoice

                           28                                  ->                       Collections

                           29                                  ->                       Event P&L

                           30                                  ->                       Financial Close

                           31                                  ->                       Feedback / Complaint

                           32                                                           Repeat Customer



      62. Architecture Decisions - DO NOT CHANGE WITHOUT APPROVAL
       Frozen Decision                                              Rule

       DECISION 01                                                  Booking, Event and Meal Session are separate objects.

                                                                    Item and Product are separate. Item = inventory/resource.
       DECISION 02
                                                                    Product = dish/sellable output.

       Frozen Decision                                          Rule

       DECISION 03                                              Recipe connects Product to Items.

                                                                Standard Cost, Estimated Cost and Actual Cost remain
       DECISION 04
                                                                separate.

                                                                Outside foods/services use one universal outsourced-order
       DECISION 05
                                                                engine; no separate Pulka/Ice Cream/Bobbatlu CRM.

       DECISION 06                                              Stock can only change through Stock Movements.

       DECISION 07                                              Inventory reservation is compulsory for confirmed events.

       DECISION 08                                              Event/session drives operational costing.

       DECISION 09                                              Event and General Overhead costs are kept separate.

       DECISION 10                                              Financial close is the final lock.

                                                                No important business transaction can bypass audit and
       DECISION 11
                                                                permissions.

                                                                Every dashboard number must be traceable to source
       DECISION 12
                                                                transactions.

                                                       CMF #01
                COMPANY / BRAND / BRANCH / KITCHEN / STORE
         Legal entity -> customer-facing brand -> operating branch -> production kitchen -> stock holding location

       Status                                                      STRUCTURE FROZEN
       Rule                                                        No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                   owner has not supplied it.



     Frozen Hierarchy
     Company / Legal Entity -> Brand -> Branch -> Kitchen -> Store / Warehouse -> optional Zone / Rack / Shelf / Bin



     Core Fields
       Field / Area                                                Frozen Rule / Content
       Company                                                     Internal ID, legal name, display name, company code,
                                                                   company type, registration identifiers, registered
                                                                   address, contacts, tax/compliance references, financial
                                                                   settings, bank/cash/UPI master references, branding,
                                                                   status.
       Brand                                                       Brand ID, company ID, brand name/code/type, public
                                                                   contacts, brand identity, commercial defaults,
                                                                   operational restrictions, status.
       Branch                                                      Branch ID, company ID, supported brands, branch
                                                                   name/code/type, address/map, contacts, operating
                                                                   hours, capacity references, commercial controls,
                                                                   service area, status.
       Kitchen                                                     Kitchen ID, branch ID, name/code/type, address,
                                                                   kitchen head, capacity references, shifts, kitchen
                                                                   sections, food-control settings, status.
       Store / Warehouse                                           Store ID, branch ID, optional kitchen ID,
                                                                   name/code/type, location, store manager, capacity,
                                                                   temperature controls, allowed categories,
                                                                   batch/expiry/FEFO settings, physical-count settings,
                                                                   status.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Company is the legal/accounting parent.

     2. Brand is the customer-facing commercial identity.

     3. Branch is the operational business location.

     4. Kitchen is a production unit and is separate from Store.

     5. Store is the inventory holding unit.

                                                                                                                      Page 2




MASTER 104 | S02.P002 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 2/40
     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack
     6. One Branch can contain multiple Kitchens and Stores.

     7. Architecture supports approved shared Branch/Kitchen use across multiple Brands.

     8. Every stock balance belongs to a valid physical or logical stock location.

     9. No transactional history is deleted when a master is inactivated or archived.

     10. Sensitive changes are audited and may require approval.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Actual legal entity details and registrations.
          Actual Brand names/settings.
          Actual Branch/Kitchen/Store list.
          Tax registrations and invoice-series mapping.
          Actual capacity values and service-radius rules.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                             Frozen Rule / Content
         ORG-01                                                   Company, Brand, Branch, Kitchen and Store are
                                                                  separate masters.
         ORG-02                                                   Brand-to-Branch relationship supports shared
                                                                  operations where approved.
         ORG-03                                                   Kitchen and Store are never treated as the same
                                                                  object.
         ORG-04                                                   Stock cannot exist outside a valid stock location.
         ORG-05                                                   Historical master references are preserved.

                                                        CMF #02
                                               CUSTOMER MASTER
       Customer account -> contacts -> communication channels -> addresses -> billing/tax profile -> preferences ->
                                               consent -> credit/risk -> Customer 360

       Status                                                      STRUCTURE FROZEN
       Rule                                                        No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                   owner has not supplied it.



     Core Objects
       Field / Area                                                Frozen Rule / Content
       Customer Account                                            Permanent customer identity; individual,
                                                                   family/household, corporate, institution,
                                                                   religious/trust/temple, public body or agency customer
                                                                   type.
       Contact Person                                              Multiple people per customer with relationship/role,
                                                                   primary/decision-maker/billing/event-coordination
                                                                   flags.
       Contact Channel                                             Multiple phone/email/WhatsApp records with type,
                                                                   primary flag, verified state and channel-specific do-
                                                                   not-contact controls.
       Address                                                     Multiple customer addresses with type,
                                                                   billing/primary flags and optional map coordinates.
       Billing / Tax Profile                                       Billing name/address, GST/tax identifiers where
                                                                   applicable, invoice email, PO-required flag, payment
                                                                   terms and credit days.
       Preferences                                                 Structured food/service/communication preferences
                                                                   and restricted dietary/allergen notes.
       Consent                                                     Marketing and transactional communication controls
                                                                   with source, date/time, proof and withdrawal history.
       Credit / Risk                                               Credit allowed/limit/days, holds, blacklist/risk flags,
                                                                   reasons, evidence, review and approval.
       Customer 360                                                Derived history and metrics from enquiries,
                                                                   quotations, bookings, events, invoices, collections,
                                                                   complaints and feedback.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Customer Account is the permanent parent identity.

     2. Contacts, phones, emails and addresses are child records and may be multiple.

     3. One customer can transact across multiple Brands and Branches without duplicate customer creation.

     4. Original acquisition source remains on Customer; each enquiry keeps its own source/campaign.

     5. Outstanding balance is finance-derived and is not manually edited in Customer Master.
                                                                                                                     Page 4




MASTER 106 | S02.P004 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 4/40
     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack
     6. Historical quotations/contracts/invoices preserve customer snapshots.

     7. Duplicate merge preserves old IDs and full transaction history.

     8. Marketing consent and operational communication are separate controls.

     9. Sensitive dietary/risk data is access-controlled.

     10. Customer Master stays separate from Enquiry, Booking, Event and Finance transactions.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Actual customer segments/tags.
          Actual credit limits/days by customer class.
          Actual consent wording/channels.
          Actual blacklist/risk approval policy.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                           Frozen Rule / Content
         CUS-01                                                 Customer Account is permanent identity.
         CUS-02                                                 Customer contacts/channels/addresses are separate
                                                                child objects.
         CUS-03                                                 Customer 360 values are derived.
         CUS-04                                                 No hard-delete of transactional customer history.
         CUS-05                                                 Cross-brand/cross-branch customer reuse is
                                                                supported.

                                                  VENUE MASTER
                                Reusable venue profile and site-survey controls for event execution

       Status                                                     STRUCTURE FROZEN
       Rule                                                       No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                  owner has not supplied it.



     Purpose
     Prevent repeated venue discovery and capture operational constraints before event execution.



     Core Fields
       Field / Area                                               Frozen Rule / Content
       Identity                                                   Venue ID, venue name, venue type, active/inactive
                                                                  status.
       Location                                                   Address, area, city, district, state, PIN, map
                                                                  coordinates, distance reference from serving
                                                                  kitchen/branch where used.
       Contacts                                                   Venue contact person(s), phone, alternate contact,
                                                                  emergency/security contact where relevant.
       Access & Timing                                            Entry gate, loading route, loading/unloading window,
                                                                  lift/service lift, parking, vehicle restrictions, event
                                                                  access time.
       Kitchen / Cooking                                          Kitchen available, cooking allowed, LPG/gas allowed,
                                                                  electrical load, induction/electric cooking suitability,
                                                                  prep/washing area.
       Utilities                                                  Water source, water availability, drainage/washing,
                                                                  power points, generator need/availability.
       Service Area                                               Dining capacity, buffet space, live-counter area,
                                                                  seating/service layout notes.
       Waste & Cleaning                                           Waste-disposal point, cleaning area, venue
                                                                  restrictions.
       Safety / Restrictions                                      Fire/safety restrictions, venue permissions, noise/time
                                                                  restrictions, restricted areas.
       Evidence                                                   Site photos, layout files, documents, previous-event
                                                                  notes, survey date, surveyed by.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Venue Master is reusable across multiple events.

     2. Event stores a venue snapshot/reference so historical execution data remains stable.

     3. A site survey is versioned by date; the latest survey does not erase previous survey evidence.

     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack
     4. Venue-specific event instructions belong to Event/BEO, not the permanent Venue Master.

     5. Venue address is not automatically treated as Customer Address.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Actual venue types.
          Whether venue survey is mandatory for every new venue or only above a threshold.
          Required survey checklist by event size/type.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                         Frozen Rule / Content
         VEN-01                                               Venue is a reusable master.
         VEN-02                                               Site survey is versioned.
         VEN-03                                               Venue constraints remain separate from event-specific
                                                              instructions.
         VEN-04                                               Venue history is retained.

                                                        CMF #04
                                                       ITEM MASTER
     Central inventory master for everything purchased, stored, consumed, issued, returned, wasted, sold or tracked
                                                            as stock

       Status                                                     STRUCTURE FROZEN
       Rule                                                       No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                  owner has not supplied it.



     Item Identity & Classification
       Field / Area                                               Frozen Rule / Content
       Identity                                                   Item ID/SKU, item name, alternate/local-language
                                                                  name(s), item type, category/subcategory, active
                                                                  status.
       Inventory Nature                                           Raw material, semi-finished, finished stock,
                                                                  disposable, cleaning, beverage, fuel/gas, outside-food
                                                                  stock item, equipment/rental stock item where
                                                                  applicable.
       UOM                                                        Purchase UOM, stock UOM, recipe UOM, conversion
                                                                  reference.
       Cost                                                       Latest price, valuation method reference,
                                                                  freight/landed-cost treatment, cost history reference.
       Yield                                                      Cleaning loss %, edible yield %, cooking yield %,
                                                                  standard waste where applicable.
       Stock Control                                              Reorder level, safety stock, batch/lot required, expiry
                                                                  required, FEFO/FIFO/weighted-average policy
                                                                  reference, negative-stock allowed = default NO.
       Storage                                                    Allowed stores/locations, temperature/storage notes.
       Food / Compliance                                          Veg/non-veg classification, allergen flags,
                                                                  food-safety/storage notes where applicable.
       Vendor Link                                                Preferred vendor(s) and approved vendor-item
                                                                  relationship reference.
       Audit                                                      Created/updated by, timestamps, status history.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Each inventory-tracked thing has one controlled Item ID.

     2. Duplicate spellings/names do not create parallel items; use aliases/local names.

     3. Stock quantity cannot be typed directly into Item Master.

     4. Every stock change occurs through a Stock Movement transaction.

     5. Item master stores policies and defaults; actual stock is by location/batch.

     6. Product/Dish and Item are separate concepts.
                                                                                                                      Page 8




MASTER 110 | S02.P008 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 8/40
     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack
     7. Historical transactions preserve the Item ID even if the item becomes inactive.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Complete catering item list.
          Actual SKU coding convention if a preferred pattern exists.
          Reorder/safety-stock values.
          Valuation policy by category.
          Actual yield percentages and storage rules.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                            Frozen Rule / Content
         ITM-01                                                  Item is the central stock identity.
         ITM-02                                                  Item and Product are separate.
         ITM-03                                                  Stock is location-based, never a master field.
         ITM-04                                                  UOM conversion is controlled centrally.
         ITM-05                                                  No silent stock edits.

                                                        CMF #05
                                                CATEGORY MASTER
                  Controlled classification for items, products, vendors, expenses and other configurable groups

         Status                                                    STRUCTURE FROZEN
         Rule                                                      No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                   owner has not supplied it.



     Scope
         Field / Area                                              Frozen Rule / Content
         Category ID                                               Permanent internal ID.
         Domain                                                    Item / Product / Vendor / Expense / Equipment / other
                                                                   approved domain.
         Parent Category                                           Optional parent for hierarchical categorization.
         Name                                                      Display name and optional alternate-language name.
         Code                                                      Optional controlled code.
         Sort / Display                                            Sort order, active/inactive status.
         Rules                                                     Domain-specific default settings or restrictions where
                                                                   explicitly configured.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Category structures are configurable and must not require code changes for normal additions.

     2. A category can have subcategories.

     3. Category deletion is not allowed when referenced; use inactive/archive.

     4. Category changes do not rewrite historical transaction classification snapshots where preserved.

     5. Item categories and Product categories are separate domains even if names look similar.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Final category tree for grocery, vegetables, fruits, dairy, non-veg, disposables, cleaning, fuel/gas, equipment,
           products and expenses.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                              Frozen Rule / Content
         CAT-01                                                    Categories are configurable masters.
         CAT-02                                                    Parent-child hierarchy is supported.
         CAT-03                                                    Domain separation prevents mixing
                                                                   Item/Product/Expense categories.

                                                        CMF #06
                                    UOM & CONVERSION MASTER
                               Purchase unit -> stock unit -> recipe unit with controlled conversions

         Status                                                    STRUCTURE FROZEN
         Rule                                                      No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                   owner has not supplied it.



     Core Fields
         Field / Area                                              Frozen Rule / Content
         UOM                                                       UOM ID, name, symbol, dimension/type such as
                                                                   weight, volume, count, pack.
         Base Unit                                                 Base unit for each dimension where applicable.
         Conversion                                                From UOM, to UOM, multiplier/divisor, effective dates,
                                                                   status.
         Item-Specific Conversion                                  Supports pack/tin/bag/case conversions that vary by
                                                                   item.
         Precision                                                 Allowed decimal precision/rounding rule.
         Audit                                                     Who changed the conversion, when, old/new value,
                                                                   reason/approval where required.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Global conversions are used only when universally valid.

     2. Item-specific pack conversions override generic pack assumptions.

     3. Purchase, stock and recipe UOMs remain separately identified.

     4. Historical recipe/purchase snapshots preserve the conversion used at that time.

     5. Conversion changes are audited because they can alter costing and stock.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Approved UOM list.
          Actual bag/tin/case/box conversions per item.
          Rounding precision by unit type.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                              Frozen Rule / Content
         UOM-01                                                    UOM is a controlled master.
         UOM-02                                                    Item-specific conversions are supported.
         UOM-03                                                    Conversion history is preserved.
         UOM-04                                                    No free-text unit entry in transactions once master is
                                                                   active.

                                                        CMF #07
                                          PRODUCT / DISH MASTER
                        Sellable or serviceable menu output linked to recipe/BOM or outsourced fulfillment

         Status                                                   STRUCTURE FROZEN
         Rule                                                     No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                  owner has not supplied it.



     Core Fields
         Field / Area                                             Frozen Rule / Content
         Identity                                                 Product ID/code, product/dish name, alternate name,
                                                                  category, veg/non-veg classification, active status.
         Fulfilment Type                                          In-house prepared, outsourced, hybrid, service-only
                                                                  where approved.
         Recipe Link                                              Approved Recipe/BOM version for in-house prepared
                                                                  product.
         Serving Unit                                             Plate, portion, piece, tray, kg, litre or other controlled
                                                                  UOM.
         Cost Views                                               Standard cost reference, estimated event cost, actual
                                                                  event cost are separate and derived/linked.
         Pricing                                                  Selling-price list references; Product Master does not
                                                                  force one global selling price.
         Production                                               Default batch/portion settings, preparation
                                                                  section/kitchen reference where configured.
         Food Controls                                            Allergen/dietary labels and service notes where
                                                                  applicable.
         Lifecycle                                                Draft/active/inactive/archived and versioned changes
                                                                  where needed.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Product is not stock unless explicitly modelled as a finished-stock Item.

     2. Product may link to one or more approved recipe versions over time.

     3. Standard, estimated and actual costs must never overwrite each other.

     4. Price may vary by Brand/Branch/Menu/Quotation and is not a single permanent value.

     5. Outsourced products may use the Outsource engine instead of in-house recipe production.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Final product/dish catalogue.
          Brand-specific product availability.
          Default serving units.

                                                                                                                       Page 12




MASTER 114 | S02.P012 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 12/40
     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack
          Default production buffers and price lists.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                    Frozen Rule / Content
         PRD-01                                          Product and Item are separate.
         PRD-02                                          Product can be in-house or outsourced.
         PRD-03                                          Cost layers remain separate.
         PRD-04                                          Historical product/recipe versions are preserved.

MASTER 115 | S02.P013 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 13/40

                                                        CMF #08
                                                RECIPE / BOM MASTER
                      Product -> ingredients/items/sub-recipes -> standard quantity/yield/batch/portion rules

         Status                                                   STRUCTURE FROZEN
         Rule                                                     No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                  owner has not supplied it.



     Core Fields
         Field / Area                                             Frozen Rule / Content
         Recipe Identity                                          Recipe ID, Product ID, version, status, effective date,
                                                                  approved by.
         Batch Definition                                         Standard batch size, standard portions/output,
                                                                  production UOM.
         Ingredient Lines                                         Item/sub-recipe, quantity, recipe UOM, yield/waste
                                                                  treatment, sequence/operation notes.
         Sub-Recipe                                               Reusable gravy, masala, dough, chutney, sauce, sweet
                                                                  base or other intermediate production recipe.
         Yield                                                    Expected edible/cooking/output yield and standard
                                                                  production loss where applicable.
         Buffer                                                   Configurable production buffer reference; no
                                                                  unsupplied percentage assumed.
         Cost                                                     Standard ingredient cost derived from item cost and
                                                                  usable quantity.
         Approvals                                                Chef/Kitchen approval and controlled version release.
         History                                                  Old recipe versions retained for historical event
                                                                  costing.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Standard Quantity, Planned Quantity and Actual Quantity are separate.

     2. Recipe version used for a confirmed quotation/event is preserved as a snapshot/reference.

     3. Changing a recipe creates a new version or controlled effective-date change; it does not rewrite history.

     4. Sub-recipes are reusable and independently versioned.

     5. Actual consumption comes from stock/production transactions, not from editing recipe lines after the event.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Actual recipes and quantities.
          Actual yields.
          Production buffer policy.
          Chef approval hierarchy.

MASTER 117 | S02.P015 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 15/40
     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack

                                                       CMF #09
                                                   MENU MASTER
            Reusable menu catalogue/package composed of products/dishes; session and brand/branch applicability
                                                           supported

         Status                                                 STRUCTURE FROZEN
         Rule                                                   No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                owner has not supplied it.



     Core Fields
         Field / Area                                           Frozen Rule / Content
         Identity                                               Menu ID/code, menu name, menu type, Brand/Branch
                                                                applicability, active status.
         Menu Type                                              Veg, non-veg, mixed, custom, package, seasonal,
                                                                religious/special-purpose or other configured type.
         Product Lines                                          Product ID, section/course/category, optional
                                                                quantity/serving rule, sort order, optional/mandatory
                                                                flag.
         Pricing Reference                                      Package/price-list reference; quotation may override
                                                                through approved commercial workflow.
         Validity                                               Effective dates/version where menus change over
                                                                time.
         Visibility                                             Internal, sales catalogue, customer/public catalogue as
                                                                configured.
         Notes                                                  Service notes, inclusions/exclusions, customer-facing
                                                                description.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Menu is a commercial/service composition; it is not inventory.

     2. Menu lines reference Products, not raw Items.

     3. Custom event menus are stored as versioned event/quotation snapshots.

     4. Public menu data must not expose internal cost/margin/stock.

     5. Menu changes do not retroactively alter confirmed historical quotations/events.

          Chart of Accounts, payment accounts, tax profiles, cost centres, invoice series and accounting mappings

       Status                                                   STRUCTURE FROZEN
       Rule                                                     No business-specific value is assumed where the
                                                                owner has not supplied it.



     Core Masters

       Chart of Accounts                                        Account ID/code/name/type, parent account, active
                                                                status.
       Cash / Bank / UPI                                        Approved cash, bank, UPI, card/gateway accounts and

       Vendor Payable                                           Vendor subledger relationship.

                                                                Kitchen optional, Event, Meal Session sub-dimension,

                                                                approved methods.

                                                                ledger entries.
       Periods / Locks                                          Financial period, close/lock status and reopen control.
       Reconciliation                                           Bank/UPI import/reference and matching rules.



     Frozen Accounting Principles
     1. Customer advance is cash/liability until recognized according to the approved accounting policy; it is not
        automatically revenue.

     2. Vendor/staff advance is not automatically an expense.

     3. Invoice, receipt, credit note, refund, vendor bill, payment and expense create ledger postings through controlled
        rules.

     4. Outstanding receivable/payable is derived from posted transactions.




                                                                                                                     Page 36




MASTER 138 | S02.P036 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 36/40
     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack
     5. Event profitability is a management view derived from revenue/cost allocations; it does not replace the
          accounting ledger.

     6. Posted/closed-period changes require controlled reversal/reopen, not direct edits.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Actual Chart of Accounts.
          Actual GST/tax profiles and statutory configuration - accountant verification required.
          Actual invoice series/numbering.
          Bank/UPI/gateway accounts.
          Posting-rule mapping and financial close policy.
          Overhead allocation policy.

     Freeze Decisions

         FIN-01                                                   Double-entry ledger layer underlies finance totals.
         FIN-02                                                   Advances are separate from revenue/expense.

         FIN-04                                                   Cost centres link operations to finance.
         FIN-05                                                   Tax/statutory values are configurable and accountant-

       Module Access                                            Sales, Events, Food, Purchase, Inventory, People,
                                                                Logistics, Finance, Marketing, Control.
       Assignments                                              User-role assignments with company/branch/event
                                                                scope and effective dates.
       Delegation                                               Temporary delegated approver with start/end dates
                                                                and allowed actions/limits.
       Audit                                                    Role/permission changes, actor, before/after, reason,
                                                                approval.



     Approval Master
       Field / Area                                             Frozen Rule / Content
       Approval Policy                                          Policy ID, process, trigger, amount/condition, scope,
                                                                required approver role/user, sequence.
       Limits                                                   Configurable limits by role, branch, category or
                                                                process.
       Separation of Duties                                     Requester/receiver/verifier/payer separation where
                                                                applicable.
       Escalation                                               SLA/due time and escalation chain reference.
       Backup Approver                                          Delegation/fallback path for unavailable approver.
       Evidence                                                 Required document/reason before approval.
       Outcome                                                  Approve/reject/request-change with audit and source-
                                                                record linkage.



     Frozen Rules
     1. Permissions are enforced by backend/API, not only hidden menus/buttons.

                                                                                                                  Page 38




MASTER 140 | S02.P038 | PDF 02 | SOURCE 38/40
     Catering CRM + ERP | All CMF Frozen Master Pack
     2. Action permissions and data visibility scopes are separate.

     3. One person must not request, receive, verify and pay the same purchase where segregation is required.

     4. Sensitive approvals use configurable limits; no amount thresholds are invented here.

     5. Delegation is time-bound and audited.

     6. System Admin technical power does not automatically grant business-payment approval.

     7. Every approval links back to the source record and retains actor/time/reason/evidence.

     OWNER CONFIRMATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

          Final role list and actual user assignments.
          Approval amount thresholds and process-specific limits.
          Escalation SLAs.
          Delegation rules.
          Sensitive-field visibility matrix.

     Freeze Decisions
         Field / Area                                           Frozen Rule / Content
         ROL-01                                                 Backend authorization is mandatory.
         ROL-02                                                 Action and scope permissions are separate.
         ROL-03                                                 Approval limits are configurable.
         ROL-04                                                 Segregation of duties is enforced where required.
         ROL-05                                                 Delegation and role changes are audited.

          NO-ASSUMPTION / OWNER CONFIRMATION REGISTER

     Not Frozen as Values
          Actual company/legal/tax registrations and invoice series.
          Actual Brand, Branch, Kitchen, Store and Venue lists.
          Final customer segment, credit-limit and blacklist policy values.
          Complete Item, Product, Menu and Recipe catalogues.
          Actual UOM pack conversions, yields and production-buffer percentages.
          Actual reorder levels, safety stock, valuation policy and physical-count frequencies.
          Actual vendor rate cards, payment terms, MOQ/lead times and required documents.
          Actual outsource catalogue, default rates, advance/transport rules and proof requirements.
          Actual staff roles, salary/rates, attendance rules, documents and payroll policy.
          Actual vehicle/equipment lists, capacity values, maintenance intervals and recovery policy.
          Actual booking advance schedule, tentative-hold expiry, cancellation/refund terms and contract method.
          Actual event/menu/headcount freeze times and capacity-override thresholds.
          Actual live-count billing rule and session type list.
          Actual expense category tree, approval thresholds and evidence requirements.
          Actual Chart of Accounts, tax configuration, bank/UPI/gateway details and posting mappings.
          Actual role assignments, approval limits, delegation rules and escalation SLAs.



     Final Freeze Statement
     FROZEN: The structure, object boundaries, relationships, control principles, audit rules and separation between
     master data and transactions for CMF #01 to CMF #20.

     NOT INVENTED: Business-specific values that were not supplied by the owner. These remain configurable and
     must be filled only from owner/accountant/operations input.

                                    MASTER DATA FREEZE 01
                              Company -> Brand -> Branch -> Kitchen -> Store / Warehouse


            Status                         FROZEN MASTER


            Version                        1.0


            Scope                          Company, Brand, Branch, Kitchen, Store / Warehouse


            Purpose                        Lock the core organization and inventory-location hierarchy before database
                                           and UI implementation.



      Freeze principle: These five masters define who owns the business, which brand sells, where operations happen,
      where food is produced, and where stock physically or logically exists.

      Kitchen: Central Veg Kitchen

      3. COMPANY RULES - FROZEN
      1. Every Brand belongs to exactly one Company.

      2. Every Branch ultimately belongs to one Company.

      3. Financial transactions must know their Company.

      4. Invoice numbering cannot cross legal entities unless explicitly configured.

      5. GST/tax settings belong primarily to the legal entity or tax registration.

      6. Company deletion is forbidden after business transactions exist.

      7. Company changes must be audited.

      8. Sensitive company tax/bank changes require authorized approval.

      5. BRAND RULES - FROZEN
      1. Brand must always belong to a Company.

      2. Brand cannot own accounting independently unless mapped to the proper legal entity.

      3. Brand revenue and cost reports must be separately available.

      4. Customer can have bookings across multiple Brands.

      5. The same Vendor can supply multiple Brands.

      6. Items may be shared or Brand-restricted.

      7. Recipes may be global or Brand-specific.

      8. Brand-specific selling prices are allowed.

      9. Brand-specific logos/documents must not retroactively change finalized historical invoices.

      10. Archived Brand remains visible in historical records.

      7. BRANCH RULES - FROZEN
      1. Every Event belongs to an operating Branch.

      2. Every employee has a home Branch unless centrally assigned.

      3. Inventory stock always belongs to a physical stock location under a Branch.

      4. Customer itself need not permanently belong to one Branch.

      5. Booking is assigned to a Branch.

      6. Event may be transferred to another Branch only through an approved transfer.

      7. Branch transfer must retain history.

      8. Branch-specific price lists are allowed.

      9. Branch-specific approval limits are allowed.

      10. Branch reporting must include Revenue, Cost, Stock, Staff and Profit.

      9. KITCHEN RULES - FROZEN
      1. Production Batch must belong to a Kitchen.

      2. One Event Session can receive products from multiple Kitchens.

      3. Kitchen is not equal to Store.

      4. Raw material must move into Kitchen through Stock Movement.

      5. Production output must create proper production inventory/consumption records where applicable.

      6. Kitchen transfer must be auditable.

      7. Veg/Non-Veg restrictions cannot be bypassed without authorized configuration.

      8. Kitchen closure/inactivation cannot remove historical production data.

      Store represents an actual stock-holding location.

        - Branch ID

      13. STOCK LOCATION SUB-STRUCTURE
      Optional detailed hierarchy:
      Store -> Zone -> Rack -> Shelf -> Bin

      Example: Main Grocery Store -> Zone A -> Rack 03 -> Shelf 02 -> Bin 05

      This is optional for small operations, but the architecture must support it.


      14. STORE RULES - FROZEN
      1. Stock cannot exist without a Stock Location.

      2. Store balance cannot be directly typed or edited.

      3. Every increase/decrease comes from a Stock Movement.

      4. Transfer between Stores creates an Out movement and an In movement.

      5. Event Venue stock is treated as a valid temporary location.

      6. Vehicle/Transit can be a temporary inventory location.

      7. Quarantine stock cannot be issued normally.

      8. Expired stock cannot be issued normally.

      9. Scrap/Waste stock cannot return to usable inventory without an approved reversal.

      10. Physical count variance requires an adjustment transaction and approval.


      15. LOCATION HIERARCHY - FROZEN
      Company -> Brand -> Branch -> Kitchen / Warehouse -> Store -> Zone -> Rack -> Shelf -> Bin

      Temporary operational locations:

        - Vehicle / Transit

        - Event Venue

        - Return Area

        - Quarantine

        - Waste / Scrap


      16. COST CENTRE LINKING


     Catering CRM + ERP - Master Data Freeze 01                                               Page 12




MASTER 154 | S03.P012 | PDF 03 | SOURCE 12/16
        Company                                 Highest accounting entity.

        Brand                                   Profitability reporting dimension.

        Branch                                  Primary operational cost centre.

        Kitchen                                 Optional cost centre.

        Event                                   Direct project/event cost centre.

        Meal Session                            Operational cost sub-dimension.

        General Overhead                        Non-event expense cost centre.




      17. ID STRUCTURE
      Recommended display IDs:

        - Company: COM-0001

        - Brand: BRD-0001
        - Branch: BRN-0001

        - Kitchen: KIT-0001

        - Store: STR-0001

      Database should use permanent unique internal IDs. Display codes may change with approval; internal
      identity must not change.


      18. DUPLICATE PREVENTION
      Company Checks
        - Legal Name

        - Registration Number

        - GSTIN

        - PAN

      Brand Checks
        - Company + Brand Name

      Branch Checks
        - Company + Branch Code

        - Address similarity

      Kitchen Checks
        - Branch + Kitchen Name

      Store Checks
        - Branch + Store Code

      System should warn before duplicate creation.

      19. MASTER STATUS RULE
      All five master types support:

        - Draft

        - Active

        - Temporarily Inactive

        - Archived

      Do not hard-delete records used in transactions.


      20. APPROVAL CONTROL
      Sensitive master changes can require approval.

        - Company GST change

        - Company tax profile

        - Bank mapping
        - Branch transfer

        - Invoice series

        - Kitchen type

        - Store item restrictions

        - Store negative-stock policy

        - Brand pricing profile


      21. AUDIT REQUIREMENT
        - Record ID

        - Changed By

        - Role

        - Old Value

        - New Value
        - Date

        - Time

        - Device

        - Reason

        - Approval Reference


      22. ROLE VISIBILITY
        Owner / CEO                             All Companies / Brands / Branches.

        Operations                              Branches, Kitchens and Stores.

        Finance                                 Company, Brand and Branch financial mappings.




     Catering CRM + ERP - Master Data Freeze 01                                                 Page 14




MASTER 156 | S03.P014 | PDF 03 | SOURCE 14/16
        Store Manager                           Assigned Stores.

        Kitchen Head                            Assigned Kitchens and relevant Stores.

        Staff                                   Only assigned operational location where necessary.

        System Admin                            Technical setup; no automatic business financial approval.




      23. MAIN RELATIONSHIP RULE
      Company: 1 -> Many Brands

      Brand: Many -> Many Branches where shared branches are required

      Branch: 1 -> Many Kitchens

      Branch: 1 -> Many Stores

      Kitchen: 1 -> Many Sections

      Store: 1 -> Many Stock Locations


      24. IMPORTANT DESIGN DECISION
      Brand and Branch should not be hard-coded as one-to-one.

      Example: one Hyderabad Central Kitchen may serve multiple approved Brands. Therefore architecture must allow
      one operational Branch/Kitchen to serve multiple Brands while every transaction still records the responsible
      Brand/Event.


      25. TRANSACTION LINKING RULE
      Purchase
      Company + Branch + Store + Event/Overhead + Brand where applicable.

      Production
      Company + Branch + Kitchen + Event + Session + Product.

      Sale
      Company + Brand + Branch + Customer + Booking + Event.


      26. FREEZE DECISIONS
          Decision            Frozen Rule

          F-01                Company is the legal/accounting parent.

          F-02                Brand is the customer-facing commercial identity.

          F-03                Branch is the operational business location.

          F-04                Kitchen is the production unit.

          F-05                Store is the inventory holding unit.

          F-06                Kitchen and Store are different objects.



     Catering CRM + ERP - Master Data Freeze 01                                                                 Page 15




MASTER 157 | S03.P015 | PDF 03 | SOURCE 15/16
          Decision            Frozen Rule

          F-07                One Branch can contain multiple Kitchens and Stores.

          F-08                Architecture supports multiple Brands using an approved shared Branch/Kitchen.

          F-09                Every stock balance belongs to a physical/logical Stock Location.

          F-10                No transactional history is deleted when a master becomes inactive.

      TWF #01 TO #15 - FROZEN TRANSACTION WORKFLOW
                            PACK
       Lead -> Quote -> Booking -> Event -> Demand -> Purchase -> Inventory -> Production -> Outsourcing ->
                          Live Event -> Staff -> Logistics -> Finance -> Close -> Feedback


          Status                           FROZEN WORKFLOW PACK


          Scope                            15 end-to-end transaction workflows for the frozen Catering CRM + ERP architecture.


          No-Assumption Rule               Unknown business-specific values are not invented. They are marked OWNER
                                           CONFIGURATION REQUIRED.


          Purpose                          Lock transaction objects, statuses, hand-offs, validations, approvals, audit rules and closing
                                           conditions before database/API/UI implementation.




      GLOBAL TRANSACTION RULES - FROZEN
      1. Every transaction has a unique immutable internal ID.

      2. Every event-related transaction links to Event ID and Meal Session where applicable.

      3. General business transactions use an approved Overhead / Cost Centre instead of a fake Event ID.

      4. No direct stock edit, direct balance edit, silent payment edit or silent closed-event edit is allowed.

      5. Create, Submit, Verify, Approve, Reject, Pay, Reopen and Cancel are separate permission actions.

      6. All important calculations are backend-authoritative.

      7. Historical commercial and cost snapshots are preserved.

      8. Failed or duplicate submissions must be idempotent and must not create duplicate financial/stock effects.

      9. Every workflow supports attachments/evidence where the business process needs proof.

      10. Every exception requires reason, actor, timestamp and approval where configured.

      11. Unknown thresholds, limits, rates, SLAs and approval values remain configurable and are never invented in this freeze.


      OWNER CONFIGURATION REQUIRED - GLOBAL
       - Discount approval thresholds and approval hierarchy by amount/percentage.
       - Advance percentage / minimum booking amount.
       - Quotation validity period.
       - Tentative booking hold duration.
       - Cancellation / postponement / refund policy.
       - Guest-count freeze deadline.
       - Production buffer rules.
       - Purchase approval limits and emergency-purchase limits.



    Catering CRM + ERP - TWF #01 to #15 Frozen Workflow Pack                                                                                Page 1




MASTER 159 | S04.P001 | PDF 04 | SOURCE 1/27
       - Stock variance / wastage approval thresholds.
       - Credit limits / credit days by customer class.
       - Payroll / overtime rules and temporary-staff rate cards.
       - Tax / GST / statutory configuration.
       - Event closing SLA and materiality threshold for late bills.
       - Notification channels and escalation timings.




    Catering CRM + ERP - TWF #01 to #15 Frozen Workflow Pack           Page 2




MASTER 160 | S04.P002 | PDF 04 | SOURCE 2/27
                      TWF #01 - LEAD / ENQUIRY FLOW
        Capture every enquiry once, qualify it, follow it up, and convert it into a quotation or a documented loss.

      STATUS: FROZEN TRANSACTION WORKFLOW


      Transaction Objects
       - Lead / Enquiry
       - Follow-up Activity
       - Lead Assignment
       - Lead Source / Campaign Reference
       - Lost Reason
       - Customer Link / Customer Creation Decision


      Required Core Data
       - Enquiry ID
       - Customer ID if existing
       - Prospect contact details if new
       - Brand
       - Branch
       - Event type
       - Preferred date
       - Venue/area
       - Expected guests
       - Food preference
       - Budget range if provided
       - Lead source
       - Campaign/UTM if available
       - Sales owner
       - Priority
       - Next follow-up
       - Notes
       - Attachments


      Frozen Status Flow
      New -> Assigned -> Contacted -> Requirement Captured -> Qualified -> Quotation Ready -> Converted

      Alternate terminal statuses: Lost, Duplicate, Invalid/Spam.


      Frozen Rules
      1. Duplicate customer/contact search runs before new Customer creation.

      2. Lead source is captured per enquiry even if the Customer already exists.



    Catering CRM + ERP - TWF #01 to #15 Frozen Workflow Pack                                                     Page 3




MASTER 161 | S04.P003 | PDF 04 | SOURCE 3/27
      3. Next follow-up is mandatory for active leads unless intentionally parked.

      4. Lost leads require a structured lost reason.

      5. Converted lead links to the created quotation/booking chain; it is not deleted.

      6. One enquiry can create multiple quotation versions but remains one enquiry.


      Approvals / Exceptions
       - Lead reassignment may follow Sales Manager permissions.
       - Duplicate merge uses Customer Master merge rules.
       - OWNER CONFIGURATION REQUIRED: lead inactivity SLA and escalation timing.




    Catering CRM + ERP - TWF #01 to #15 Frozen Workflow Pack                               Page 4




MASTER 162 | S04.P004 | PDF 04 | SOURCE 4/27
                                     TWF #02 - QUOTATION
       Create controlled, versioned commercial offers with menu, price, margin checks, extras, tax settings and
                                               customer acceptance.

      STATUS: FROZEN TRANSACTION WORKFLOW


      Transaction Objects
       - Quotation
       - Quotation Version
       - Quotation Line
       - Menu/Package Snapshot
       - Discount Request
       - Customer Approval / Acceptance


      Quotation Components
       - Customer / Booking context
       - Brand / Branch
       - Event / session assumptions
       - Guest count
       - Menu/package
       - Per plate / package / fixed pricing
       - Live counters
       - Transport
       - Rentals
       - Service staff
       - Outside food/services
       - Discount
       - Tax profile
       - Payment schedule
       - Terms
       - Validity
       - Estimated cost snapshot
       - Expected margin


      Frozen Status Flow
      Draft -> Internal Review -> Approval Required -> Approved -> Sent -> Viewed/Delivered -> Accepted

      Alternate statuses: Rejected, Expired, Superseded, Cancelled.


      Frozen Rules
      1. Every commercial change creates a new quotation version; prior versions remain readable.



    Catering CRM + ERP - TWF #01 to #15 Frozen Workflow Pack                                                Page 5




MASTER 163 | S04.P005 | PDF 04 | SOURCE 5/27
      2. Accepted quotation is snapshotted and cannot be silently overwritten.

      3. Estimated cost and margin snapshot at quote time are preserved.

      4. Low-margin or high-discount quotation triggers configured approval.

      5. Customer acceptance must preserve who accepted, when, which version and evidence/reference.

      6. Final invoice can differ only through approved extras/changes, not silent quote edits.

       - Quotation validity days
       - Discount thresholds
       - Target margin rules
       - Default advance rule
       - Tax configuration
       - E-sign/acceptance method

      Convert an accepted commercial offer into a controlled booking with advance, contract, schedule and event

      STATUS: FROZEN TRANSACTION WORKFLOW

       - Booking Payment Schedule

       - Advance Receipt Link

       - Cancellation / Postponement Record


      Frozen Status Flow
      Tentative -> Advance Pending -> Confirmed -> Planning -> Completed

      Frozen Rules
      1. Booking is the commercial parent and may contain one or more Events.

      4. Confirmed booking reserves required operational capacity/resources through downstream modules.

      5. Cancellation and postponement never delete the original booking; they create auditable outcome records.

      6. Customer balance is finance-derived, not manually typed into Booking.

            Convert confirmed event menus and guest counts into consolidated ingredient, consumable and

      STATUS: FROZEN TRANSACTION WORKFLOW


      Transaction Objects
       - Demand Run
       - Demand Requirement Line
       - Inventory Reservation
       - Shortage Line
       - Planner Override


      Frozen Formula
      Net Requirement = Planned Requirement + Safety/Buffer - Available Stock - Confirmed Incoming Stock


      Frozen Rules
      1. Demand is generated from frozen recipe/menu versions and current confirmed guest counts.

      2. Demand can be viewed by Event, Session, Kitchen, Item, Product and consolidated date range.

      4. Planner may override generated quantity only with reason.

      5. Demand changes after event/menu/headcount change must re-evaluate reservations and purchase shortages.

      6. Demand planning can consolidate multiple events into one procurement requirement.

                                      TWF #06 - PURCHASE
           Control procurement from requirement to approved vendor payment with quantity, quality and rate
                                                    verification.

      STATUS: FROZEN TRANSACTION WORKFLOW


      Transaction Objects
       - Purchase Requirement
       - Purchase Request
       - Vendor RFQ / Quote
       - Quote Comparison
       - Purchase Order
       - GRN
       - Vendor Bill
       - Payment Approval


      Frozen Status Flow
      Requirement -> PR Submitted -> Quote Comparison -> PO Approved -> Ordered -> Partially Received ->
      Received -> Bill Verified -> Paid


      Frozen Rules
      1. Purchase lines link to Event/Session or approved Overhead allocation where applicable.

      2. Requester, approver, receiver and payer permissions are separated.

      3. PO changes after vendor acceptance create revision history.

      4. GRN captures ordered, delivered, accepted, rejected and short quantity.

      5. Three-way match compares PO, accepted GRN and vendor bill.

      6. Rate/quantity/tax/freight mismatch can hold payment.

      7. Emergency purchase requires reason, buyer, evidence, event/overhead allocation and approval path.

      8. One purchase/bill may allocate to multiple Events or Overheads through allocation lines.

       - Payment approval limits

         Provide one controlled money flow for customer advances, invoices, vendor bills, receipts, payments,

      1. Customer advance is not automatically revenue.

      5. Cash, Bank, UPI, Card and Gateway are separate accounts/channels.

MASTER 180 | S04.P022 | PDF 04 | SOURCE 22/27
                               TWF #14 - EVENT CLOSING
          Prevent premature closure by reconciling stock, staff, vendor costs, customer extras, collections and
                                                      profitability.

      STATUS: FROZEN TRANSACTION WORKFLOW


      Frozen Closing Stages
      1. Operational Close - service execution complete.

      2. Return Close - stock, containers and equipment reconciled.

      3. Staff Close - attendance, OT and temporary staff settlement verified.

      4. Vendor Cost Close - outside food, purchase, rental and logistics bills verified or marked expected.

      5. Customer Close - final guest count, approved extras and billing basis confirmed.

      6. Provisional P&L; - remaining expected/missing costs visible.

      7. Financial Close - material costs/revenue complete and event locked.


      Frozen Closing Blockers
       - Unresolved stock return/variance
       - Missing critical vendor bills or expected-cost treatment
       - Unverified staff hours/settlement
       - Unbilled approved customer extras
       - Unreturned equipment / unresolved damage
       - Unresolved payment/reconciliation issue
       - Required incident/complaint action still open


      Frozen Rules
      1. Operational completion cannot directly mark Financially Closed.

      2. Late bill after close requires controlled reopen or approved post-close adjustment policy.

      3. Final Event P&L; uses actual cost where available and clearly marks provisional cost if not final.

      4. Reopening records reason, approver, old close state and new changes.

      5. Closed event source transactions remain auditable and cannot be silently edited.


      Owner Configuration Required
       - Closing SLA
       - Late-bill materiality policy
       - Who can reopen
       - Allowed post-close adjustments

      CROSS-WORKFLOW INTEGRATION FREEZE
                                            How the 15 frozen workflows must connect

      MASTER FLOW
      Lead -> Quotation -> Booking -> Event/Session -> Demand -> Purchase/Inventory -> Production ->
      Outsourcing -> Live Event -> Staff/Logistics -> Finance -> Event Close -> Feedback/Repeat


      MANDATORY LINKAGE MATRIX
       Field                               Frozen Rule

       Lead                                Customer / prospect identity, Brand, Branch, source.

       Quotation                           Lead/Customer + Brand/Branch + event/session assumptions + cost snapshot.

       Booking                             Customer + accepted quotation + commercial commitments.

       Event/Session                       Booking + operational branch/kitchen/venue + guest/menu data.

       Demand                              Event/Session + Recipe/Product + Item requirements.

       Purchase                            Demand/Event/Overhead + Vendor + Store destination.

       Inventory                           Item + Location + Event/Session/source transaction.

       Production                          Kitchen + Event/Session + Product + ingredient movements.

       Outsourcing                         Event/Session + Vendor + outsourced item/service.

       Live Operations                     Event/Session + count/counter/extra/incident.

       Staff                               Person + Branch/Kitchen/Event/Session + attendance/settlement.

       Logistics                           Event/Session + Vehicle/Driver + Load/Equipment.

       Finance                             Customer/Vendor/Staff + Event/Overhead allocation + ledger.

       Event Close                         All required operational and financial source records.

       Feedback                            Customer + Event/Session + resolution/finance links.



      CALCULATION SEPARATION - FROZEN
       - Standard Cost != Estimated Event Cost != Actual Event Cost.
       - Expected Guests != Guaranteed Guests != Final Confirmed Guests != Actual Served.
       - Ordered Qty != Delivered Qty != Accepted Qty != Returned/Short Qty.
       - On Hand != Reserved != Available != Incoming.
       - Invoice Value != Collected Cash != Outstanding.
       - Operational Complete != Financially Closed.


      AUDIT & APPROVAL - FROZEN
       - Sensitive approval uses source record, actor, role, amount/impact, reason, evidence and timestamp.
       - Rejection preserves request history.
       - Reopen never erases prior closed state.



    Catering CRM + ERP - TWF #01 to #15 Frozen Workflow Pack                                                           Page 25




MASTER 183 | S04.P025 | PDF 04 | SOURCE 25/27
       - Before/after values are preserved for material changes.
       - Exports of sensitive finance/customer data are permission-controlled and auditable.


      NO-ASSUMPTION REGISTER
      The following were intentionally NOT invented in this workflow freeze:

       - Exact rupee approval limits
       - Exact discount percentages
       - Exact advance percentage
       - Exact guest buffer percentage
       - Exact quotation validity days
       - Exact tentative-hold hours/days
       - Exact cancellation/refund percentages
       - Exact purchase quote count
       - Exact price variance tolerance
       - Exact stock variance tolerance
       - Exact wastage tolerance
       - Exact event close deadline
       - Exact staff wage/OT rates
       - Exact mileage/fuel reimbursement
       - Exact GST/tax interpretation
       - Exact reminder/escalation times
       - Exact complaint compensation limits

MASTER 186 | S05.P001 | PDF 05 | SOURCE 1/36
                                                  PART A - DSRF
                                               Database Schema & Relationship Freeze

      DSRF STATUS: FROZEN


      A1. Database Design Principles
      1. One authoritative database serves web, mobile and reporting; no duplicate business database.

      2. Every business entity has an immutable internal primary key. Human-readable codes are separate display identifiers.

      3. Foreign keys enforce ownership relationships; important relationships are not stored only as free text.

      4. Transactional history is not hard-deleted through normal application workflows.

      5. Stock balances, customer outstanding, vendor payable and P&L; are derived from authoritative transactions or controlled
      materialized summaries, not manually maintained totals.

      6. Every event-related operational/financial row links to Event ID and Meal Session when applicable; general costs use an
      approved Overhead/Cost Centre.

      7. Standard, estimated and actual costing data are stored separately.

      8. Commercial and operational snapshots preserve historical values after master data changes.

      9. Concurrency-sensitive reservations/confirmations use database transactions and uniqueness/locking controls.

      10. Audit and approval history are append-oriented and cannot be silently replaced.


      A2. Naming & Key Conventions
       Convention                      Frozen Rule

       Primary key                     UUID/opaque internal ID recommended; never business name as key.

       Display code                    Human-readable unique code, e.g. CUS-000001, EVT-000001; format configurable.

       Foreign key                     _id pattern in logical model.

       Money                           Fixed decimal numeric type; never binary floating point.

       Quantity                        Decimal numeric with UOM.

       Date/time                       Store timestamp with timezone handling; event local timezone preserved.

       Status                          Controlled enumerated/reference value; not arbitrary free text.

       Soft lifecycle                  status / inactive_at / archived_at instead of normal hard delete.

       Versioning                      version_no + effective timestamp + previous/reference where history is required.

       Audit stamps                    created_at/by, updated_at/by; sensitive changes additionally in audit log.

      A21. Database Constraints - FROZEN
       - Unique normalized customer phone/email constraints are warning/controlled uniqueness where shared family/business
         contacts make strict uniqueness unsafe.

       - Unique display codes per configured scope.
       - No stock movement with zero quantity unless explicit correction type allows it.
       - No negative money totals on normal invoices/bills except dedicated credit/reversal documents.
       - No cross-company finance posting without explicit inter-company design; not assumed in this freeze.
       - Closed event records reject unauthorized edits at backend and database/service validation layers.
       - Approval source record cannot change critical values while approval is pending without invalidating/restarting approval.
       - Inventory reservations cannot exceed permitted available/override logic without recorded exception.
       - Foreign-key delete actions default to RESTRICT for live transactional relationships.
       - Every financial/stock multi-row write runs in one transaction to prevent partial posting.
      OWNER CONFIGURATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

       - Exact database engine and hosting choice.
       - Display code formats/sequences.
       - Data-retention years for non-statutory records.
       - Multi-company/inter-company accounting if ever required.
       - Exact tax registration structure if multiple GST registrations exist.
       - Performance partitioning thresholds based on actual volume.
