                                                         PART B - PRF
                                                              Permission & Role Freeze

      PRF STATUS: FROZEN


      B1. Permission Actions
       Code                Action

       V                   View

       C                   Create

       E                   Edit

       S                   Submit

       X                   Verify

       A                   Approve/Reject

       P                   Pay/Post Financial

       R                   Reopen/Reverse

       D                   Soft-delete/Archive where allowed

       O                   Export/Download sensitive data


      A hidden menu is not security. Every backend write/read/export must enforce permission + data scope + approval
      limit.


      B2. Frozen Business Roles
       Role                                     Frozen Scope

       Owner / CEO                              Company-wide visibility and final/high-value approvals; routine data entry not required.

       COO / Operations Head                    Cross-event operations, capacity, closing and exceptions.

       Finance Head                             Finance verification, limits, reconciliation, P&L;, payroll totals.

       Accountant                               Invoices, receipts, bills, reconciliation preparation; cannot self-approve sensitive payments.

       Sales Manager                            Leads, customers, quotations, bookings, customer due within allowed visibility.

       Sales Executive                          Assigned leads/customers/quotes/bookings; restricted cost/profit visibility.

       Purchase Manager                         Requirements, vendors, RFQ, PO preparation and approved buying.

       Store Manager                            GRN, stock, issues, returns, counts, expiry, wastage.

       Kitchen Head / Chef                      Recipes, production plans, prep, quality, stock requests.

       Event Manager                            Assigned event/session, BEO/checklists, live count, extras, incidents, operational close.

       HR Manager                               Employee, roster, attendance, payroll preparation.

       Logistics Manager                        Vehicles, trips, equipment, loading, return.

       Worker / Staff                           Own assigned duties, attendance, tasks, own payslip where applicable.

       Driver                                   Own trip, route, loading/proof, trip status; no commercial profit view.

       Marketing / CRM                          Consent-compliant segments/source attribution; no finance editing.

       System Admin                             Technical users/settings/backups/integrations; no business payment approval by default.

       Customer risk/blacklist                  Sales Manager, Finance, Owner; restricted from broad staff.

       Customer consent/contact export          Marketing/Sales only within permitted scope; export audited.

       Vendor bank/payment details              Finance + authorized Purchase; restricted elsewhere.

       Audit logs                               Owner/System Admin/Auditor by scope; not broadly editable.

       Sensitive Data                           Frozen Visibility Rule

       System secrets                           Never exposed to normal business UI.



      B5. Segregation of Duties
       Process                             Frozen Separation

       Purchase                            Requester != receiver != bill verifier != payer for sensitive/high-value flow.

       Stock adjustment                    Counter/Store submits; configured supervisor approves material variance.

       Refund                              Accounts prepares; Finance verifies; authorized approver approves; payment posted
                                           separately.

       Payroll                             Attendance verified -> HR calculates -> Finance verifies -> authorized approval -> payment.

       Customer discount                   Sales requests; configured manager/owner approves above limit.

       Event reopen                        Operations/Accounts request; authorized role approves; audit captures reason.

       Vendor master bank change           Creator/editor cannot silently cause payment destination change without controlled verification.

       Role/permission change              System Admin may configure; business owner authorizes sensitive access policy.


      OWNER CONFIGURATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

       - Exact approval limits by role/module.
       - Whether branch managers exist as separate role in final org.
       - Delegation/backup approver assignments.
       - Exact export permissions.
       - Whether MFA is mandatory for all users or only privileged roles.

                                                   PART C - CRF
                                                            Calculation Rules Freeze

      CRF STATUS: FROZEN


      C1. Quantity & Inventory Calculations
       Metric                           Frozen Definition

       On Hand                          Opening migration quantity + all approved inbound movements - all approved outbound
                                        movements.

       Reserved                         Sum of active inventory reservations not released/consumed.

       Available                        On Hand - Reserved.

       Incoming                         Approved open PO/transfer quantities not yet received, subject to configured inclusion rule.

       Net Demand                       Planned requirement + configured buffer/safety stock - Available - eligible confirmed incoming.

       Actual Consumption               Event/Kitchen issue + approved direct-event purchase - usable return to stock, adjusted for
                                        production transfers where applicable.

       Consumption Variance             Actual Consumption - Standard/Ideal Consumption.

       Physical Variance                Physical Count - System Quantity at count cut-off.

       Wastage Value                    Approved wastage quantity x valuation cost at configured method.



      C2. UOM & Yield Calculations
       Metric                           Frozen Definition

       Base Quantity                    Transaction Qty x approved UOM conversion factor.

       Usable Quantity                  Received/processed quantity x actual or standard usable-yield ratio depending on stage.

       Usable Unit Cost                 Landed input cost / usable quantity.

       Recipe Scaled Qty                Recipe base qty x required portions / recipe base portions.

       Planned Qty                      Scaled recipe qty adjusted by approved event/production buffer.

       Yield Variance                   Actual usable/output qty - standard expected usable/output qty.



      C3. Purchase & Landed Cost
       Metric                           Frozen Definition

       Line Gross                       Ordered/accepted quantity x unit rate.

       Line Discount                    Configured vendor discount calculation.

       Tax                              Configured tax engine; not hard-coded in this freeze.

       Freight/Other                    Allocated freight/handling/other landed charges.

       Landed Cost                      Net item value + allocated non-recoverable charges/taxes as configured accounting policy.

       Price Variance                   Current effective rate - comparison/base rate.

       GRN Variance                     Delivered/accepted quantity vs PO quantity.

       Bill Match Variance              Vendor bill values vs accepted GRN/PO according to match policy.




    Catering CRM + ERP - Complete Implementation Freeze Pack                                                                              Page 14




MASTER 199 | S05.P014 | PDF 05 | SOURCE 14/36
      C4. Product / Recipe Costing
       Metric                                Frozen Definition

       Standard Ingredient Cost              Standard recipe quantities x standard/effective costing rate chosen by policy.

       Standard Product Cost                 Standard ingredient cost + configured standard direct production allocations.

       Estimated Event Food Cost             Event planned quantities x quote/planning cost snapshot.

       Actual Food Cost                      Actual consumed quantities x actual inventory/direct-purchase valuation.

       Actual Cost per Portion               Actual batch/product cost / actual approved portions/output quantity.

       Estimated Margin                      Quoted net selling value - estimated event cost.

       Actual Contribution                   Event revenue - actual direct event cost.

       Full Net Profit                       Event contribution - allocated overhead.

       Food Cost %                           Food cost / applicable net selling revenue x 100.

       Margin %                              Margin / net selling revenue x 100.


      Standard Cost, Estimated Event Cost and Actual Event Cost are separate values and must never overwrite one
      another.


      C5. Customer Commercial Calculations
       Metric                              Frozen Definition

       Quotation Subtotal                  Sum of commercial lines before discount/tax.

       Discount                            Configured line/header discount subject to approval.

       Taxable / Net Base                  Calculated by configured tax profile; exact GST treatment OWNER CONFIGURATION
                                           REQUIRED.

       Invoice Total                       Net commercial base + configured taxes/charges - credits.

       Paid Amount                         Valid posted receipts allocated to booking/invoice/advance.

       Customer Balance                    Final invoice/receivable amount - allocated receipts - approved credit notes + valid debit
                                           adjustments.

       Advance Available                   Posted customer advance not yet consumed/refunded.

       Overdue Days                        Current local date - due date for unpaid due amount; floor at zero.



      C6. Vendor & Staff Payables
       Metric                              Frozen Definition

       Vendor Payable                      Approved vendor bills - allocated vendor payments - vendor credit notes.

       Vendor Advance Available            Posted vendor advance - amount applied/returned.

       Temporary Staff Gross               Approved base rate calculation + OT + allowances + reimbursements.

       Temporary Staff Net Payable         Gross - advances - approved deductions.

       Payroll Net                         Approved gross components - advances/loans - deductions/statutory deductions per
                                           configured policy.



      C7. Logistics & Equipment Calculations


    Catering CRM + ERP - Complete Implementation Freeze Pack                                                                            Page 15




MASTER 200 | S05.P015 | PDF 05 | SOURCE 15/36
       Metric                              Frozen Definition

       Trip Cost                           Fuel/reimbursement + toll + parking + driver/vehicle charges + approved extras.

       Equipment Outstanding               Issued quantity/assets - valid returned quantity/assets.

       Damage/Missing Recovery             Approved recoverable amount linked to responsible party; no automatic amount invented.

       Transport Charge to Customer        Commercial quote/extra line; independent from internal trip cost.



      C8. Event Closing & Profit
       Metric                              Frozen Definition

       Event Revenue                       Approved final invoice revenue basis excluding pass-through tax according to accounting
                                           configuration.

       Direct Food Cost                    Actual food consumption + approved direct food purchases + outside food cost.

       Direct Labour                       Temporary labour + allocated event labour according to configured policy.

       Direct Logistics                    Trip/transport/rental/equipment direct costs.

       Other Direct Cost                   Packing, disposables, event-specific fees and approved direct expenses.

       Event Contribution                  Event Revenue - all direct event costs.

       Allocated Overhead                  Configured overhead allocation run.

       Net Event Profit                    Event Contribution - Allocated Overhead.

       Provisional Profit                  Event P&L; containing identified expected/missing costs; clearly labeled provisional.

       Final Profit                        P&L; after Financial Close or approved post-close adjustment policy.



      C9. Rounding & Currency Rules
       - All stored money uses fixed-decimal precision; display rounding is separate from calculation precision.
       - Rounding occurs at explicitly defined accounting/tax stages; UI must not independently round and re-post totals.
       - Quantity precision is item/UOM-specific and configurable.
       - Currency is stored with transaction/company context; multi-currency is not assumed unless configured later.
       - Every computed dashboard KPI can be reconciled to source transactions.
      OWNER CONFIGURATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

       - GST/tax formulas and rounding policy.
       - Inventory valuation method by category: weighted average/FIFO/other.
       - Overhead allocation method.
       - Permanent labour allocation method.
       - Customer extra-plate commercial pricing rule.
       - Production buffer/safety stock percentages.
       - Payroll/OT formulas.

                                                               Screen / UI Freeze

      UIF STATUS: FROZEN MINIMUM SCREEN INVENTORY


      D1. Global UI Rules
      1. One consistent design system across web and Android; role changes available actions, not the meaning of status colors.

      2. Every list supports search, relevant filters, sort, pagination/infinite loading and export only where permission allows.

      3. Every detail screen shows source ID, status, owner, timestamps, linked records and audit/approval status where relevant.

      4. Create/Edit forms validate before submit and show server validation errors next to fields.

      5. Destructive/reversal/reopen actions require reason and confirmation.

      6. Every KPI/drill-down opens the exact source list or record; no dead summary numbers.

      7. Financially closed records are read-only except authorized reopen/adjustment flow.

      8. Mobile venue workflows prioritize large touch targets, offline state, sync status and proof capture.

MASTER 207 | S05.P022 | PDF 05 | SOURCE 22/36
                                                       PART E - APIF
                                                                  API Contract Freeze

      APIF STATUS: FROZEN LOGICAL CONTRACT


      E1. API Architecture
      1. One versioned HTTPS JSON API serves web and Android clients.

      2. Business calculations, permissions and workflow transitions are server-authoritative.

      3. API version prefix is explicit, e.g. /api/v1/.

      4. Authentication/session/token implementation may follow chosen stack, but every private request is authenticated.

      5. Authorization checks role + action + data scope + approval limit on every protected endpoint.

      6. Write requests that can create money/stock/booking effects support idempotency keys.

      7. Concurrent edit protection uses record version/updated timestamp or equivalent optimistic concurrency control.

      8. List endpoints support pagination, filtering, sorting and server-side search.

      9. Errors use stable machine-readable error codes plus human-readable messages.

      10. File uploads are separate controlled multipart/upload flow with type/size validation.


      E2. Standard API Response Envelope
      { "data": ..., "meta": { ... }, "errors": [] }

       - Successful single-resource responses return data object.
       - List responses return data array plus pagination meta.
       - Validation/business errors return field/source references where possible.
       - Server never exposes stack traces or secrets to client.

      E3. HTTP / Error Semantics
       Status                 Frozen Meaning

       200                    Successful read/update/action.

       201                    Resource created.

       202                    Accepted async operation where used.

       400                    Malformed request / invalid syntax.

       401                    Not authenticated.

       403                    Authenticated but not authorized.

       404                    Resource not found or not visible in scope.

       409                    Conflict: duplicate, stale version, double booking, invalid state transition.

       422                    Business/field validation failed.

       429                    Rate limit.

       500                    Unexpected server error with trace/reference ID only.



      E4. Frozen Resource Endpoint Families

       Reminder                          /{id}/acknowledge, /snooze, /complete



      E6. Idempotency & Concurrency
       - Idempotency key mandatory for payment, receipt, stock movement, GRN posting, invoice finalization, event close/reopen
         and other configured critical writes.

       - Reusing the same idempotency key with a different payload is rejected.
       - Stale record version on update returns conflict; client reloads current record before retry.
       - Resource reservation/confirmation executes atomically to prevent double booking of stock/equipment/vehicle/capacity.
       - API retries must never duplicate journal lines, receipts, stock movements or external payment effects.

      E7. Validation & Security Contract
       - Backend validates required fields, numeric bounds, status transitions, UOM compatibility, ownership scope and duplicate
         rules.

       - Client-submitted calculated totals are treated as display hints at most; server recalculates authoritative totals.
       - Uploads validate extension, MIME/content, size and access scope.
       - Exports require explicit permission and are audited.
       - Private responses containing sensitive data use no-store/no-cache policy as appropriate.
       - Every API error logs server trace/reference without exposing secrets to user.
      OWNER CONFIGURATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

       - Exact authentication technology and session lifetime.
       - Exact API pagination size defaults/maxima.
       - Exact upload limits and allowed file types.
       - Rate limits by endpoint/user.
       - External integrations/payment gateways/WhatsApp provider.

      4. Reports distinguish standard/estimated/actual and provisional/final values.

      5. Exports preserve applied filters and generation timestamp.

      6. Sensitive reports and exports are audited.

       - Company

       - Meal Session
       - Product
       - Item
       - Category
       - Date range
       - Status
       - Owner/employee
       - Payment status
       - Cost centre
       - Provisional vs final where applicable

      F3. Product-wise / Item-wise Drilldown - FROZEN
      Product -> Recipe Version -> Ingredient Lines -> Demand -> Purchase/Stock -> Actual Consumption ->
      Wastage -> Actual Cost -> Event/Session -> Revenue -> Contribution

      Item -> Opening/Receipts -> Reservations -> Issues -> Consumption -> Returns -> Wastage -> Closing ->
      Cost -> Vendor/Price History -> Events Used

      TAF STATUS: FROZEN


      G1. Test Layers
       Test Layer                      Frozen Coverage

       Unit                            Calculations, validation, state transitions, permission helpers.

       Integration                     DB + service + API + accounting/stock posting.

       Workflow/E2E                    Lead through event financial close and repeat follow-up.

       Permission                      Every role/action/scope, including direct API attempts.

       Security                        Auth, session, upload, injection, rate limit, secrets, export controls.

       Concurrency                     Double booking, duplicate payment, simultaneous stock/GRN/approval writes.

       Mobile                          Offline queue, sync, camera/proof, back navigation, reconnect.

       Performance                     Large customers/items/events/movements/reports and peak event-day usage.

       Recovery                        Backup restore, migration rollback/repair, failed integration.

       User Acceptance                 Real business scenarios with owner/department sign-off.



      G2. Mandatory Business Test Scenarios
      1. Existing customer creates new enquiry without duplicate customer record.

      2. Quotation revision preserves old version and accepted version snapshot.

      3. High discount is blocked until required approval.

      4. Tentative booking cannot permanently consume resources beyond hold policy.

      5. Two users cannot confirm the same unavailable equipment/vehicle/capacity concurrently.

      6. Event with multiple sessions maintains separate guest count/menu/cost while rolling to booking totals.

      7. Demand excludes stock already committed to other events.

      8. PO -> partial GRN -> vendor bill mismatch holds payment as configured.

      9. Emergency purchase allocates to correct event/overhead and creates evidence/audit.

      10. Stock issue/return/wastage reconciles to movement ledger and balance.

      11. Expired/quarantine stock cannot be normally issued.

      12. Recipe plan and actual consumption remain separate and variance calculates correctly.

      13. Outsource ordered/delivered/accepted/returned quantities do not collapse into one field.

      14. Live count corrections retain history and do not overwrite prior entries.

      15. Customer extra follows approval and reaches final invoice.

      16. Temporary staff advance reduces settlement payable correctly.

      17. Equipment missing at return remains outstanding and links to incident/recovery.

      18. Customer advance increases cash but not earned event revenue automatically.

      19. Vendor advance does not become expense automatically.

      20. Receipt allocation updates outstanding without manually editing customer balance.



    Catering CRM + ERP - Complete Implementation Freeze Pack                                                        Page 31




MASTER 216 | S05.P031 | PDF 05 | SOURCE 31/36
      21. Financial close is blocked while required closing blockers remain.

      22. Authorized reopen preserves prior close history.

      23. Late vendor bill follows configured post-close/reopen policy.

      24. Event P&L; reconciles to food, labour, logistics, outsource, expenses and revenue.

      25. Unauthorized user calling API directly receives denial even if UI button is hidden.

      26. Duplicate idempotency key cannot create second receipt/payment/stock movement.

      27. Offline mobile entries sync once and resolve/flag conflicts without duplication.

      28. Restore from backup reproduces critical master, transaction, audit and finance integrity.


      G3. Definition of Done - Release Gate
      1. All frozen CMF/TWF/DSRF relationships implemented or explicitly approved as deferred.

      2. Critical calculations pass automated tests with agreed test fixtures.

      3. Role/permission matrix passes UI and direct API tests.

      4. No critical/high security issue remains open.

      5. No unreconciled duplicate posting bug in finance/stock workflows.

      6. Android/web critical workflows pass on representative real devices/browsers.

      7. Backup and restore drill succeeds on non-production environment.

      8. Migrations are repeatable and preserve data.

      9. Owner configuration values required for go-live are supplied and documented.

      10. UAT sign-off exists for Sales, Operations, Store/Purchase, Kitchen, Finance and Owner flows.

MASTER 217 | S05.P032 | PDF 05 | SOURCE 32/36
                                                   PART H - SRF
                                            Security, Offline, Backup & Reliability Freeze

      SRF STATUS: FROZEN TECHNICAL CONTROL REQUIREMENTS


      H1. Authentication & Authorization
       - Every private API requires authenticated session/token.
       - Privileged accounts support/require MFA according to owner configuration.
       - Server authorization is mandatory on read, write, approve, pay, reopen, export and admin actions.
       - Session expiry/revocation is enforced server-side.
       - Password/API/signing secrets are never stored in source code or client logs.

      H2. Data & Application Security
       - HTTPS only in production.
       - Input/schema validation on every write.
       - Parameterized ORM/query practices; no string-built SQL from user input.
       - Upload validation by type, size and content where feasible.
       - Sensitive logs are redacted.
       - Private customer/finance responses are not indexed/cached publicly.
       - Critical exports are permissioned, watermarked/logged if configured and audited.
       - Audit log records sensitive view/change/export/payment/approval actions according to policy.

      H3. Mobile Offline Freeze
      1. Offline read cache is encrypted/protected according to platform capability and contains only role-scoped data.

      2. Only explicitly allowed actions can be queued offline.

      3. Queued actions carry client-generated request ID/idempotency key.

      4. User can see Pending Sync, Synced, Conflict and Failed states.

      5. Conflict does not silently overwrite newer server data.

      6. Financial close, sensitive approval and configured high-risk actions may require online connection.

      7. Logout/session revocation clears or invalidates protected local data according to security policy.


      H4. Backup & Recovery Freeze
       - Automated encrypted backups with documented retention.
       - Pre-migration backup before production schema change.
       - Restore procedure tested regularly on non-production environment.
       - Backup includes database and required file/object attachments or references.
       - Recovery objectives are explicitly configured before go-live.
       - Backup success/failure is monitored and alerted.

      H5. Monitoring & Reliability
       - Health checks for API/database/background jobs/storage.
       - Error/crash monitoring with trace/reference IDs.


    Catering CRM + ERP - Complete Implementation Freeze Pack                                                              Page 33




MASTER 218 | S05.P033 | PDF 05 | SOURCE 33/36
       - Queue/integration failure monitoring.
       - Critical alerting for backup failure, payment/stock posting errors, sync backlog and security anomalies.
       - Database migrations are versioned and reviewable.
       - No destructive automated testing against production.
      OWNER CONFIGURATION REQUIRED - NOT INVENTED

       - MFA policy.
       - Session timeout.
       - Backup frequency/retention.
       - RPO and RTO values.
       - Production hosting/provider.
       - External logging/monitoring provider.
       - Exact data-retention/privacy policy.




    Catering CRM + ERP - Complete Implementation Freeze Pack                                                        Page 34

     Catering CRM + ERP | PDF 06 - Purchase & Expense Master Freeze Pack




      1. Freeze Scope & Non-Negotiable Rules
      This pack consolidates the purchase master lists, folder structure, search/scroll behaviour, staff-wise purchase tracking,
      outside purchases and additional expense sections requested for the catering CRM/ERP.
      1       One Item Master record per real item. Different spellings, singular/plural forms and local names become aliases, not
              new item rows.
      2       Purchase transactions can be unlimited. Duplicate transactions are different from duplicate master items.
      3       Normal stock purchases must ultimately flow through Purchase/GRN/Stock Movement. Do not create a parallel stock
              database.
      4       Outside ready-made food/services use the existing universal Outsource engine, not a separate hard-coded CRM for
              each food.
      5       Temporary staff payments use Staff Advance/Settlement; transport uses Logistics/Expense; utilities and rent use
              Expense/Overhead; live-counter service cost links to Event/Session where applicable.
      6       Logged-in staff identity must automatically attach to purchase transactions.
      7       All important edits, cancellations, reversals and deletions are audited. Posted stock/finance history must not
              disappear.
      8       Payment status should be Finance-derived whenever a finance transaction exists.
      9       Every Event-related purchase/expense should link to Event and Meal Session where applicable; general overhead
              uses an Overhead Cost Centre.
      10      Search must support partial matching and aliases; long lists must use a sticky search bar and vertical scrolling.

          Core architecture               Easy UI is allowed. Simplified backend is not. A user may enter a purchase from one screen, but the
          safeguard                       system must still create the correct authoritative transactions underneath.



      2. Final Navigation & Backend Routing
      2.1 Purchase Home
          Folder                                        Authoritative Backend

          Kirana                                        Inventory Purchase -> GRN -> Stock

          Vegetables / Kayagoralu                       Inventory Purchase -> GRN -> Stock

          Disposables                                   Inventory Purchase -> GRN -> Stock

          Fruits                                        Inventory Purchase -> GRN -> Stock

          Non-Veg                                       Inventory Purchase -> GRN -> Stock

          Gas / Fuel                                    Inventory Purchase / Expense based on item purpose

          Water / Ice                                   Inventory Purchase / Outsource based on configuration

          Outside Purchases                             Universal Outsource Order / Delivery / Settlement

          Other Items                                   Authorized Item Master + Inventory Purchase or Expense based on classification


      2.2 Expenses Home
          Section                                       Authoritative Backend

          Staff Advances                                Staff Advance / Settlement

          Temporary Staff Payments                      Temporary Assignment / Staff Settlement

          Cleaning Department                           Service/Expense; cleaning materials remain inventory items

          Transport Charges                             Trip / Logistics / Event Expense

      3. Master Item Governance, Duplicates & Aliases
      The system must normalize obvious duplicate names before seeding. A single master record may be visible in multiple
      folder views through category/tag links, but it keeps one Item ID.

          Duplicate / Ambiguity                      Canonical handling                  Frozen rule

          Kobbari Bondam / Tender Coconut            Tender Coconut                      Keep one Item Master row; use Kobbari Bondam as alias.

          Mosambi / Sweet Lime                       Mosambi                             Keep one Item Master row; Sweet Lime can be an alias.

          Green Chilli / Pachi Mirapakayalu          Pachi Mirapakayalu                  One master item with alias search.

          Capsicum / Green Capsicum                  Capsicum (Green)                    One default green item; Red/Yellow Capsicum stay separate.

          Aratipandlu repeated in Catering Special   Aratipandlu                         Use tag/view membership, not another master row.
          list

          Grapes + Green/Black/Seedless Grapes       Grapes + optional variants          Create variants only when the business purchases them
                                                                                         separately.

          Diesel / Generator Diesel                  Generator Diesel                    Default catering seed uses Generator Diesel; generic Diesel
                                                                                         can be added later only if needed.

          Petrol / Generator Petrol                  Generator Petrol                    Default catering seed uses Generator Petrol.

          Charcoal Bags                              Charcoal                            Use UOM = Bag; do not create a second item.

          Firewood Bundles                           Firewood                            Use UOM = Bundle; do not create a second item.

          Kerosene Can                               Kerosene                            Use UOM = Can/Litre; do not create a second item.

          White Eggs / Kodi Gudlu                    Kodi Gudlu                          Default seed treats White Eggs as alias unless owner needs
                                                                                         a distinct product.

          Ghee outside purchase / Neyyi              Neyyi                               Outside transaction links to the same Item Master row.

          Gas outside purchase                       Existing Gas/Fuel item              Outside transaction links to existing gas master identity.

          Lemon in Vegetables and Fruits             One Lemon Item Master               May appear in more than one folder view through links/tags,
                                                                                         but one item_id only.


      3.1 Duplicate Prevention on Add Item
      1        Normalize case, spaces and punctuation.
      2        Search exact name, alias, singular/plural variants and common English-letter Telugu spellings.
      3        Show likely duplicate warning before creation.
      4        Allow staff to choose an existing item; only authorized Admin/Master-Data role may create a genuinely new item.
      5        Store alternate names/aliases on the same record.
      6        Never auto-merge historical transactions without approved master-data governance.

          Search behaviour
          ●
              Instant filtering while typing; no mandatory Search button.
          ●
              Full name, starting letters and partial-word matching.
          ●
              Alias matching: e.g. Green Chilli can find Pachi Mirapakayalu; Kobbari Bondam can find Tender Coconut.
          ●
              Category heading + item name matching.
          ●
              Search result should show item name, category and allowed UOMs.
          ●
              Clear-search action returns the full scroll list.
          ●
              Sticky search remains visible while list scrolls.
          ●
              Expandable/collapsible category sections for large lists.
          ●
              Use lazy loading or list virtualization only if required for performance; it must not break search.

      14.8 Global Additional-Expense Payment Rule
      Balance = Final Payable - Total Amount Paid. Payment status values may include Unpaid, Advance Paid, Partially
      Paid, Fully Paid, Settled and Cancelled. Where Finance is linked, the status must be derived rather than freely
      typed.

      17. Event Cost / P&L Integration
      For each Event, create an Event Cost / Purchase Breakdown that groups actual direct costs without mixing them into
      general overhead.

       Cost Group                                      Examples

       Inventory Purchases                             Kirana, Vegetables, Fruits, Non-Veg, Disposables, Water/Ice, Gas




     FROZEN EXTENSION | No duplicate master items | No silent financial/stock edits                                       Page 26




MASTER 247 | S06.P026 | PDF 06 | SOURCE 26/36
     Catering CRM + ERP | PDF 06 - Purchase & Expense Master Freeze Pack




       Cost Group                                      Examples

       Outside Purchases                               Pulka, Rumali, Bobbatlu, Ice Cream, Pan, outside sweets/services

       Labour                                          Temporary Staff, Cleaning Service

       Logistics                                       Transport

       Live Counters                                   Vendor/Counter setup/service

       Other Direct Event Expenses                     Approved Event-linked additional costs

      The Event Actual Cost and Event P&L must consume these authoritative transactions. General rent, current bill,
      WiFi and similar overheads should remain in Overhead Cost Centres unless an approved allocation rule applies.


      18. Permissions, Audit, Edit/Cancel/Delete Rules
       Action                              Frozen behaviour

       Create                              Authorized role; logged-in creator captured.

       Edit Draft                          Allowed by permission; Updated By/At captured.

       Submit                              Moves record into controlled workflow.

       Approve                             Separate permission from Create/Submit.

       Cancel                              Reason required; downstream effects reversed/handled through controlled workflow.

       Delete                              No unrestricted hard delete after posting. Use soft-delete/archive only when safe and retain audit.

       Stock impact                        Cannot be silently edited; changes occur through Stock Movements/returns/adjustments.

       Payment impact                      Cannot be silently edited; use Finance reversal/adjustment workflow.

       Closed Event                        Cannot be silently edited; reopen requires permission, reason and approval.

       Attachments                         Changes audited where material.


      Audit minimum
      ●
          Created By
      ●
          Created At
      ●
          Updated By
      ●
          Updated At
      ●
          Submitted By
      ●
          Approved By
      ●
          Old Value
      ●
          New Value
      ●
          Reason
      ●
          Approval / Reversal Reference

      19. Functional Acceptance Tests / Definition of Done
      1     Search 'Kandi' returns Kandi Pappu immediately; no Search button required.
      2     Search 'pappu' returns all relevant pappu-named items including dry-fruit pappu names.
      3     Search 'Ulli' returns Ullipayalu; 'Tom' returns Tomatalu; 'Vanka' returns base and variant Vankaya records.
      4     Alias search finds Pachi Mirapakayalu when user searches Green Chilli.
      5     Master-data add warns if staff tries to create Ullipaya when Ullipayalu already exists.
      6     Lemon may be visible in more than one folder view but must have one Item ID.
      7     Staff creates a purchase; Staff ID/Name attach automatically without manual entry.
      8     One bill can contain Kirana + Vegetables + Fruits + Non-Veg + Water items under one Purchase Bill ID.
      9     Purchase save persists after refresh.
      10    Purchase submit/GRN creates correct stock movement and destination-store quantity.
      11    Partial GRN records ordered, delivered, accepted, rejected and short quantity separately.
      12    Bill photo remains linked and viewable later.
      13    Outside Pulka order records ordered/delivered/accepted/returned separately and does not create a separate Pulka
            CRM.
      14    Staff advance appears in Staff settlement, not as Kirana purchase.
      15    Rent/Current/WiFi post to Overhead Expense, not fake Event IDs.
      16    Event-linked live-counter/transport/outside-food costs appear in Event Actual Cost/P&L.
      17    Finance-derived payment status matches receipts/payments; manual free-text does not override finance.
      18    User without purchase permission cannot create/edit purchase via UI or API.
      19    Double-click submit does not create duplicate GRN, payment or stock movement.
      20    Soft delete/cancel retains audit and does not erase posted financial/stock history.
      21    Dashboard totals reconcile to purchase/expense source transactions.
      22    Staff-wise report answers: who bought what, quantity/UOM, rate, supplier, spend and dates.
      23    Item-wise report answers monthly quantity, average/latest rate, spend and staff/supplier details.
      24    Supplier report shows bills, rejections, price history, amount paid and outstanding.
      25    Search and vertical scrolling remain usable on desktop, tablet and mobile.


          Production-ready                Pages and buttons alone are not completion. Correct data + correct routing + correct workflow +
          definition                      correct permissions + correct calculations + correct audit + correct reports + passing tests are

          5. Code Cleanup & Modularity Before Transfer
          Lovable may generate working code in large page/component files. Before handover, oversized or mixed-responsibility code
          must be refactored safely without changing working behavior.
      •
             No giant App.tsx / main.tsx / index.tsx holding most business screens and rules.
      •
             Every major page/screen should have an identifiable file.
      •
             Every business feature should have an identifiable feature/module folder.
      •
             Shared UI components should be reusable.
      •
             API/integration clients should be separate from page JSX.
      •
             Business logic should not be buried inside large UI components.
      •
             Validation, types/models, state, hooks and utilities should be separated where appropriate.
      •
             Existing correct code should be moved/refactored, not unnecessarily regenerated.
      •
             Refactoring must be followed by regression tests.

          Recommended orientation
          src/
          pages/
          features/
          components/
          hooks/
          services/
          store/
          lib/
          types/
          styles/
          utils/
          tests/

          The receiving company must not search the whole repository to locate a feature. Every major feature must be mapped.

      NOT BUILT
      UI ONLY

      components

           LATER FINALIZED ADDITIONAL CRM LAYER
                       Targets • Analytics • Calendar • Purchase UI/Search • Outside Purchases • Menu PDF • UI Rules

    Finalized Points List
    1. Reference UI policy: ClickUp, Pipedrive and Zoho are visual inspiration only: layout, hierarchy, card clarity, chart placement,
    sidebar, spacing, buttons and pipeline style. Do not copy their business records, branding or proprietary workflow.
    2. Dashboard visual structure: Dashboard should give a fast owner view: major target first, KPI cards, source-driven charts,
    operational schedule/reminders/alerts and drill-down into real records.
    3. Monthly Target Board: Monthly Target must be visually large. Show Target, Achieved, Balance, Achievement %, Daily Average
    Required, Today Achieved and Remaining Days.
    4. Yearly Target: Show Yearly Target, YTD Achieved, Balance, Achievement %, average monthly achievement, required monthly
    run-rate and projected year-end result.
    5. Dynamic target logic: Achievement must come from authoritative source transactions. Valid qualifying additions increase
    achievement; approved cancellation/reversal/refund/adjustment decreases it. Do not maintain disconnected manual achievement
    totals.
    6. Target sub-goals: Allow configured Revenue, Booking, Event, New Customer, Collection, Purchase Control, Cost Control and
    Profit targets, with progress bars and drill-down.
    7. Workload by Status: Use a donut/pie chart for actual Pending/In Progress/Completed/Waiting/Overdue/etc. statuses. Clicking a
    segment opens the filtered source records.
    8. Leads by Source: Use actual configured Lead Source data in a donut chart. Clicking a source opens filtered Leads.
    9. Lead / Booking Pipeline: Visualize the frozen CRM pipeline with count, value and conversion by stage. Each stage is clickable.
    10. Performance analytics: Support 3/6/12-month and custom-date performance for Leads, Quotes, Bookings, Events, Revenue,
    Collections, Purchase Value, Direct Cost, Contribution and Outstanding.
    11. Purchase Pipeline visual: Show Demand -> Purchase Requirement -> PR -> Vendor Comparison -> PO -> Delivery -> GRN ->
    QC -> Vendor Bill -> Payment, with counts/value/pending/delayed/approval pending.
    12. Purchase category UI: Purchase Home must show separate categories: Kirana, Vegetables, Disposables, Fruits, Non-Veg,
    Gas/Fuel, Water/Ice, Outside Purchases and Other Items.
    13. Category-wise master access: All frozen items remain accessible inside their correct canonical master/category. Visual folders
    must not create duplicate Item IDs.
    14. Sticky search per purchase category: Each category has sticky live search, partial-word search, starting-letter search,
    alias/local spelling search, filters and vertical scrolling.
    15. Universal Purchase Search: Provide Search All Purchase Items across authoritative Item Master records; result shows
    category/subcategory/UOM and available latest rate/vendor context without duplicating the master.
    16. One Item = One Master ID: Aliases, alternate spellings, category views or search contexts must not create duplicate master
    rows.
    17. + Add Item control: Only authorized Admin/Master Data roles can add master items. Normalize, exact match, alias match and
    similar-name duplicate check before creation.
    18. Outside Purchase UI: Provide clean UI categories for Pulka, Chapathi, Rumali Roti, Bobbatlu, Ice Cream, Pan/Paan and other
    outside food/service while preserving one universal Outsource engine.
    19. Outside food variants: Support relevant sizes/types/variants/counts/rates; do not hard-code a closed list where business
    needs configurable variants.
    20. Ice Cream configuration: Support type, flavour, brand, quantity/UOM, bulk quantity, rate, vendor, delivery, advance, paid,
    balance and status. Vanilla/Butterscotch are examples, not a permanent closed enum.
                                        Catering CRM + ERP | Later Finalized Additional Layer + UI References




MASTER 268 | S08.P001 | Part 08 | SOURCE 1/10
    21. Outside Purchase transaction detail: Track Event/Meal Session, vendor/mobile, ordered/delivered/accepted/returned/short
    quantity, UOM/rate, transport/setup/other/discount, advance/paid/balance/status, dates, bill/proof and notes as applicable.
    22. Purchase reports: Support Today/Yesterday/Week/Month/Quarter/Year/Custom and
    Category/Item/Staff/Supplier/Event/Payment/Rate History/Outside Purchase/Pending Supplier views while preserving frozen
    reports.
    23. Purchase analytics: Spend by Category, Monthly Purchase Trend, Top Purchased Items, Supplier Spend, Paid vs Pending,
    Outside Purchase Spend, Vegetable Trend and Non-Veg Trend.
    24. No meaningless UOM totals: Never add KG + Litre + Piece into one quantity total. Group quantity reports by Item/UOM;
    financial totals may aggregate where valid.
    25. Inventory visuals: Provide On Hand, Reserved, Committed, Available, Incoming, Low Stock, Expiring and Wastage
    cards/charts with source drill-down.
    26. Event Cost visual: Show actual Kirana, Vegetables, Fruits, Non-Veg, Disposables, Gas, Water/Ice, Outside Purchase, Temp
    Staff, Cleaning, Transport, Live Counter and other direct event costs.
    27. Event P&L visual: Show Revenue -> Direct Cost -> Contribution -> Allocated Overhead -> Net Profit using frozen financial
    definitions; do not redefine the formulas.
    28. Full Calendar module: Calendar is a full module with Month, Week, Day and Agenda views.
    29. Calendar sources: Visualize existing authoritative Events, Meal Sessions, Work, Staff Assignments, Vendor/Purchase
    Deliveries, Follow-ups, Reminders, Payment Due, Vehicle/Equipment Schedule, Festivals and task due dates.
    30. Calendar confusion control: Month date cells show only 3-4 compact chips; if more, show +N more. Full information opens
    after date click.
    31. Calendar color coding: Use a consistent legend, e.g. Event blue, Meal Session cyan, Work purple, Delivery orange, Reminder
    yellow, Payment red, Festival green, Completed green tick, Overdue red.
    32. Date Details drawer: Clicking a date opens grouped Festival, Events, Meal Sessions, Work, Deliveries, Reminders, Payments
    and Vehicle/Equipment details; each entry links to its source record.
    33. Person-wise Work Calendar: Filter by employee/person/team/event/status and show assigned work, location, start/due time,
    priority and status.
    34. Work Completion: Capture Completed By, completion date/time, notes and proof where required, using existing approval rules
    when applicable.
    35. Pending Work Report: Show Person, Work, Event, Assigned Date, Due Date, Status, Priority and overdue time with
    Today/Week/Month/Person/Event/Status filters.
    36. Festival Calendar: Use configured/authoritative festival data; do not invent dates. Festival and operational Event on the same
    date must both remain visible.
    37. Dashboard Calendar Preview: Provide compact Upcoming Schedule with today's work/events, upcoming events, deliveries,
    reminders, payments due and next festivals.
    38. Menu Builder placement: Use Food & Menu -> Menu Builder / Menu Templates / Generated PDFs while preserving
    Menu/Product/Recipe/Event/Meal Session architecture.
    39. Empty branded Menu PDF template: Admin can configure Brand logo/name, contact, header/footer, background, typography,
    category/item style, page number, optional terms and placeholders.
    40. Menu PDF auto-fill: Customer -> Event -> Meal Session -> Menu -> Template -> Preview -> Generate. Reuse existing CRM
    data; avoid unnecessary re-entry.
    41. Menu PDF content: Support brand/customer/event/date/venue/session/menu
    title/categories/items/notes/reference/version/created date/footer as configured.
    42. Menu PDF actions: Preview, Save Draft, Generate PDF, Download, Share, Generate New Version and History.
    43. PDF version protection: Approved PDF versions must not be silently overwritten. Regeneration creates a new version and
    preserves history.
    44. Brand-wise templates: Allow brand-specific templates without changing Company -> Brand -> Branch hierarchy.
    45. Professional UI system: Colourful but controlled; rounded cards, clean typography, clear icons + labels, consistent status
    chips, readable tables, drawers and responsive design.
    46. Button hierarchy: Primary for Add/Create/Save/Submit/Confirm/Generate; Secondary for Edit/Preview/Filter/Export; Tertiary for
    History/Share/More; destructive actions visually distinct.
    47. Clickable analytics: Charts are operational navigation, not decoration. Important chart segments/bars/cards must open the
    relevant filtered source transactions.

    49. Existing / Partial / Missing rule: Already exists -> reuse. Partial -> complete. Missing -> add. Conflict -> frozen architecture

        profitability remains based on frozen calculations.

     Use in Existing Project                                               Do not restart or rebuild the frozen core. Inspect current

    Strict Additional-Layer Do-Not Rules
     • Do not create a new CRM/project for these additions.
     • Do not remove frozen functions, fields, workflows, statuses, calculations, reports, permissions or audit.
     • Do not create fake analytics or decorative non-source-driven numbers.
     • Do not create duplicate Item Masters, duplicate Calendar transaction copies or a second Menu database.
     • Do not create separate backends for Pulka/Chapathi/Rumali/Bobbatlu/Ice Cream/Pan.
     • Do not hard-code configurable flavours/variants as permanently closed lists.
     • Do not manually override finance-derived balances/payment status or directly edit stock balances.
     • Do not sum incompatible UOM quantities.
     • Do not invent festival dates or owner-specific tax/rate/approval values.
     • Do not overwrite approved Menu PDF versions.
     • Do not rebuild unrelated working screens merely to match a reference image.
     • Do not copy ClickUp/Pipedrive/Zoho business data, branding or UI pixel-for-pixel.

    Acceptance for This Additional Layer
     • All frozen core requirements still work after the additions.
     • Targets reconcile to source transactions, including approved reversals.
     • Charts/KPIs drill into real records.
     • Purchase search finds existing items without duplicate creation.
     • Outside purchases route through the universal Outsource workflow.
     • Calendar remains readable and links back to source records.
     • Menu PDFs use CRM data, preserve versions and remain customer-ready.
     • Desktop/tablet/mobile layouts remain usable.
     • No unexplained frozen requirement is lost.

         MASTER BUILD + CODE MODULARITY
       + APP COMPANY HANDOVER STANDARD
            Consolidated project plan, code-location discipline, stack compatibility and transferable handover



      Purpose                     One document that tells the builder/development company what must exist in the Catering CRM,
                                  how the code must be organised, how every feature's code must be located quickly, and how the
                                  complete project must be handed over to another app/software company without vendor lock-in.




      Core Rule                   No giant single-file application. Every major feature must have a clear module/file location and
                                  must be listed in a Feature-to-Code Map. Small future changes should be possible by editing the
                                  relevant module instead of searching or rewriting the whole application.




      Stack Rule                  The actual code must be handed over in the language/framework it really uses. If the receiving
                                  company wants a different language/stack, create a compatibility and migration plan; do not
                                  silently rewrite the whole system just because the target company's preferred stack is different.




                                   Catering CRM + ERP | Master Build + Code Modularity + App Company Handover




MASTER 278 | S09.P001 | Part 09 | SOURCE 1/8
     1. What This Master PDF Covers
      • The frozen Catering CRM + ERP business architecture and implementation requirements.
      • The later additional UI/UX layer: targets, analytics, diagrams, calendar, purchase navigation/search and menu PDF.
      • Source-code modularity and exact code-location documentation.
      • Actual technology-stack documentation and receiving-company compatibility/migration planning.
      • Frontend, backend, database, API, environment, deployment, credentials and testing handover.
      • A final replacement-developer test so another company can continue the project without depending on the original
        Lovable workspace.

      Final Success               A new development company should be able to open START-HERE.md, understand the stack,
      Condition                   run the project, find the exact code for any major feature, make a targeted change, test it and
                                  deploy it without calling the old developer.



     2. Complete Project Functional Plan
     2.1 Core Organisation & Master Data
      • Company / Legal Entity -> Brand -> Branch -> Kitchen -> Store / Warehouse hierarchy.
      • Customer, Venue, Item, Category, UOM & Conversion, Product/Dish, Recipe/BOM, Menu, Vendor, Outsource, Staff,
         Vehicle, Equipment/Asset, Booking, Event, Meal Session, Expense, Finance/Ledger and Role/Approval masters.
      • One real item = one Item ID. Aliases and alternate spellings must not create duplicate master records.
      • Item and Product/Dish remain separate. Recipe/BOM connects Products to Items.
      • Standard Cost, Estimated Cost and Actual Cost remain separate.

     2.2 CRM, Sales & Event Flow
      • Lead -> Contacted -> Requirement Captured -> Quotation -> Negotiation -> Advance Pending -> Confirmed -> Event
         Planning -> Event Completed -> Payment Pending -> Closed.
      • Customer -> Booking -> Event -> Sub-Event where applicable -> Meal Session.
      • Quotation/version history, customer requirements, event/venue/session linkage and controlled status transitions.
      • Event close includes operational close, returns, staff/vendor cost completion, customer/finance checks, provisional P&L
         and final financial close.

     2.3 Food, Menu, Recipe & Production
      • Menu -> Product/Dish -> Recipe/BOM -> Item/Ingredient.
      • Recipes are versioned; approved versions are traceable to production and costing.
      • Production, preparation, packing, dispatch and actual consumption must link back to Event/Meal Session where
        applicable.
      • Actual consumption and stock are transaction-derived; no free manual stock balance edits.

     2.4 Purchase, Inventory & Outsource
      • Demand -> Purchase Requirement -> PR -> Vendor Quote/Comparison -> PO -> Delivery -> GRN -> QC -> Vendor Bill
         -> Payment.
      • Stock changes only through Stock Movements.
      • Inventory supports on-hand, committed/reserved, available, incoming, low-stock, expiry/wastage and location-based
         stock.
      • Universal outsourced food/service engine for Pulka, Chapathi, Rumali Roti, Bobbatlu, Ice Cream, Pan/Paan and other
         outside services; do not build separate CRMs/databases for each.
      • Purchase categories include Kirana, Vegetables, Disposables, Fruits, Non-Veg, Gas/Fuel, Water/Ice, Outside Purchases
         and Other Items.

     2.5 Staff, Logistics, Expense & Finance
      • Staff assignments, temporary staff, advances, cleaning, transport, vehicles, equipment and live-counter costs.
                                   Catering CRM + ERP | Master Build + Code Modularity + App Company Handover




MASTER 279 | S09.P002 | Part 09 | SOURCE 2/8
      • Event-linked direct costs must flow to Event Actual Cost/P&L; Rent, Current Bill, WiFi and other general overheads
        remain overhead unless an approved allocation rule exists.
      • Finance/Ledger remains authoritative for payments, collections, outstanding and settlement status.
      • Advance is not automatically revenue; payment is not automatically expense.
      • Revenue - Direct Cost = Contribution; Contribution - Allocated Overhead = Net Profit, using the frozen financial rules.

MASTER 280 | S09.P003 | Part 09 | SOURCE 3/8
     4. Mandatory Code Architecture - No Giant Files
      Non-Negotiable              Do not place the whole application inside one App.tsx, main.tsx, index.tsx or other giant
                                  generated page. Working AI-generated code must be refactored safely into identifiable modules
                                  before final handover.


      Area                                                               Required Separation

      Pages / Screens                                                    Each major screen has an identifiable file.

      Features / Domains                                                 Each major business feature has its own identifiable
                                                                         module/folder.

      Reusable UI                                                        Buttons, cards, tables, forms, dialogs and layouts are
                                                                         shared, not copied.

      Business Logic                                                     Rules/calculations live in domain/service logic, not buried
                                                                         inside visual components.

      API / Integrations                                                 Centralised by domain; do not duplicate API calls across
                                                                         unrelated pages.

      Types / Models                                                     Shared source of truth; no repeated incompatible
                                                                         definitions.

      Validation                                                         Reusable and testable.

      State / Queries                                                    Clear ownership and predictable data flow.

      Database                                                           Schema/migrations/indexes/policies are reproducible and
                                                                         documented.

      Tests                                                              Mapped to the feature they verify.


     4.1 Change Isolation Rule
      • A small change should normally touch only the relevant feature/page/component/service and its tests/docs.
      • Do not regenerate a large working screen to add one field, one button or one validation rule.
      • Before creating new code, search for reusable components, services, types, functions, tables and APIs.
      • Refactor when responsibilities become mixed or navigation becomes risky - not only at final handover.
      • After every meaningful change, update the Feature-to-Code Map so the next developer knows where the change
        belongs.

      Why                          The receiving company must not search blindly through the repository. For every major feature,
                                   documentation must show the exact frontend, backend, database, API, permission and test
                                   locations.

     6. Code Language / Receiving Company Stack Rule
      Important                   Source-code handover means delivering the complete editable source in the
                                  language/framework the project actually uses. The receiving company's preferred language is a
                                  migration decision, not a reason to hide, convert or rewrite the existing source without control.

      • Document the actual current stack for Web, Mobile, Admin, Backend/API, Database, Auth, Realtime, Storage,
         Notifications and Hosting.
      • Lock exact important versions and package/dependency files.
      • If the receiving company uses a different language/framework, create a Stack Compatibility / Migration Matrix.
      • For every mismatch record: current stack, target stack, compatible YES/NO, migration required YES/NO, affected
         modules/files and recommended continuation approach.
      • Prefer incremental module-by-module migration when needed; do not blindly rewrite the entire application.
      • The receiving company must receive enough source, documentation and test evidence to either continue in the current
         stack or plan a controlled migration.
      Layer                       Current Actual Stack     Target Stack               Migration?                Notes

      Web                         Actual                   Receiving target           YES/NO                    Affected modules +
                                  language/framework/v                                                          migration note
                                  ersion

      Mobile                      Actual                   Receiving target           YES/NO                    Affected modules +
                                  language/framework/v                                                          migration note
                                  ersion

      Backend/API                 Actual                Receiving target              YES/NO                    Affected services +
                                  language/runtime/fram                                                         API contracts
                                  ework

      Database                    Actual DB + migration    Receiving target           YES/NO                    Schema/data
                                  layer                                                                         migration plan

     8. Functional and Handover Acceptance
      • A page/button is not completion. Save must persist, refresh must retain, correct permission must apply, backend
         transaction must be correct, calculations/reports must update and audit must be written where applicable.
      • Double-click/idempotency protection must prevent duplicate critical transactions such as GRN, payment and stock
         movement.
      • Cancellation/reversal must preserve history and reverse effects safely instead of deleting posted business history.
      • Dashboard and report totals must reconcile to source transactions.
      • Fresh-machine / replacement-developer test: clone -> configure -> run -> test core workflows -> build production.
      • If hidden setup knowledge or the old developer's explanation is required, handover is NOT ready.

     8.1 Final PASS Conditions
      Gate                                                               PASS Requirement

      Functional coverage                                                All frozen + approved additional requirements complete or
                                                                         explicitly documented.

      Code modularity                                                    No giant mixed-responsibility handover; feature locations
                                                                         are identifiable.

      Feature Code Map                                                   Every major feature has code/DB/API/permission/test
                                                                         mapping.

      Stack manifest                                                     Actual stack and versions documented.

      Compatibility                                                      Receiving-company target stack differences documented.

      Source ownership                                                   Editable source and repository access transferred.

      Database/API                                                       Reproducible database and documented API.

      Environment/credentials                                            Secure handover path documented.

      Deployment                                                         Fresh build/deploy/rollback instructions verified.

      Fresh developer test                                               PASS without old-developer dependency.

     9. How To Use This PDF in the Current Catering CRM Project
      1. Keep the existing six frozen Catering CRM PDFs as the business/functional source of truth.
      2. Use the approved additional UI/analytics/target/calendar/purchase/menu-PDF requirements as the additional layer.
      3. Use this Master Build + Code Modularity + Handover PDF during finalisation to ensure code is organised and
         transferable.
      4. Do not restart or rebuild correct working modules only to satisfy documentation; refactor only where needed and test
         after each change.
      5. Create/update the mandatory documentation while finalising the application, not after the source has already become
         difficult to understand.
      6. At handover, give the receiving company source + docs + database/API + environment/deployment instructions + test
         evidence.

     10. One-Line Execution Command for Lovable / Current Developer
      Execution Command           Continue inside the existing Catering CRM + ERP project. Preserve all frozen and correct
                                  working functionality. Complete approved missing/partial requirements, refactor only where
                                  necessary into clear modules, create START-HERE.md, TECHNOLOGY-STACK.md,
                                  PROJECT-MAP.md, FEATURE-CODE-MAP.md, STACK-COMPATIBILITY.md and the full
                                  handover documentation, then run the fresh-developer acceptance test. Do not create a new
                                  project, duplicate databases/workflows, or rewrite working modules without need.

                                            UNIVERSAL SOFTWARE PROJECT

                    SOURCE CODE ARCHITECTURE,
               MODULARITY & FINAL HANDOVER STANDARD
                                                     ATTACHMENT A - FINAL v2.0
                        For Any App, Website, Web App, Mobile App, Admin Panel, SaaS, Lovable or AI-Built Project


       Field                                                                    Final Requirement

       Use                                                                      Attach this PDF at project start to the Proposal / SRD / SOW / MSA and
                                                                                again at final handover.

       Primary Goal                                                             A replacement development company must be able to understand the
                                                                                complete project, locate every major feature code, run it, build it and
                                                                                continue development without searching blindly or depending on the
                                                                                original vendor.

       Code Rule                                                                Each major page, module, service, API, database area and business
                                                                                feature must have a documented code location. Large projects may not be
                                                                                handed over as one giant generated file.

       Stack Rule                                                               The exact language, framework, runtime and versions used in every layer
                                                                                must be declared and locked in the handover manifest.

       Ownership Rule                                                           Client-paid custom code, project files, database structures, documentation
                                                                                and transferable project assets must be handed over in
                                                                                editable/reproducible form, subject to the signed agreement and third-party
                                                                                licences.

       Lovable Rule                                                             Lovable/AI-generated code has the same handover obligations as
                                                                                manually written code. Production must not depend on hidden vendor-only
                                                                                builder access.



       Final success test                                                       A new developer opens START-HERE.md, immediately knows the
                                                                                stack, repositories, module paths, setup steps and current project
                                                                                status, then runs the project on a fresh machine and finds the exact
                                                                                code for any major feature without calling the old developer.
